R75 | Theorems and lemmas in the frozen project modules

[AffineCellCounts] KqR75.AffineCellCounts.baseline_unique
KqR75.AffineCellCounts.baseline_unique.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {C : Type u_3}
  [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] [Fintype G] [DecidableEq G] [DecidableEq C] (s : G → K)
  (tag : G → C) (ref : C) {i j : G} (hij : s i ≠ s j) (hi : tag i ≠ ref) (hj : tag j ≠ ref) (p : K × K) :
  KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s (fun x => Equiv.refl K) tag ref p = tag ↔ p = (0, 0)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[AffineCellCounts] KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram_pair_invariant
KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram_pair_invariant.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {K : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2}
  {C : Type u_3} {T : Type u_4} [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] [Fintype G] [DecidableEq G]
  [DecidableEq C] [DecidableEq T] [Fintype T] (typeOf : (G → C) → T) (a : T → ℤ) (P : Finset (G × G))
  (s : G → K) (hP : ∀ ij ∈ P, s ij.1 ≠ s ij.2) (f : C → C → ℤ)
  (ha : ∀ (labels : G → C), a (typeOf labels) = ∑ ij ∈ P, f (labels ij.1) (labels ij.2)) (tag : G → C)
  (ref : C) (σ : G → Equiv.Perm K) :
  ∑ u, KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram typeOf s σ tag ref u * a u =
    ∑ u, KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram typeOf s (fun x => Equiv.refl K) tag ref u * a u
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

KqR75.AffineCellCounts.active_subset.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {C : Type u_3} [Field K]

  KqR75.AffineCellCounts.active (KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s σ tag ref p) ref ⊆

Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[AffineCellCounts] KqR75.AffineCellCounts.eq_tag_of_card_ge
KqR75.AffineCellCounts.eq_tag_of_card_ge.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {C : Type u_3}
  [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] [Fintype G] [DecidableEq G] [DecidableEq C] (s : G → K)

    (KqR75.AffineCellCounts.active tag ref).card ≤

[AffineCellCounts] KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours.congr_simp
KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours.congr_simp.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {C : Type u_3}
  [Field K] {inst✝ : DecidableEq K} [DecidableEq K] (s s✝ : G → K) (e_s : s = s✝) (σ σ✝ : G → Equiv.Perm K)
  (e_σ : σ = σ✝) (tag tag✝ : G → C) (e_tag : tag = tag✝) (ref ref✝ : C) (e_ref : ref = ref✝) (p p✝ : K × K)
  (e_p : p = p✝) (i i✝ : G) (e_i : i = i✝) :
  KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s σ tag ref p i =
    KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s✝ σ✝ tag✝ ref✝ p✝ i✝
Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

[AffineCellCounts] KqR75.AffineCellCounts.top_difference
KqR75.AffineCellCounts.top_difference.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {K : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {C : Type u_3}
  {T : Type u_4} [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] [Fintype G] [DecidableEq G] [DecidableEq C]
  [DecidableEq T] (typeOf : (G → C) → T) (degree : T → ℕ) (ref : C)
  (hdegree : ∀ (f : G → C), degree (typeOf f) = (KqR75.AffineCellCounts.active f ref).card) (s : G → K)
  (tag : G → C) {i j r : G} (hij : s i ≠ s j) (hir : i ≠ r) (hjr : j ≠ r) (hi : tag i ≠ ref)
  (hj : tag j ≠ ref) (hr : tag r ≠ ref) (τ : Equiv.Perm K) (hτ : τ 0 ≠ 0) (u : T)
  (hu : degree (typeOf tag) ≤ degree u) :
  KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram typeOf s (fun x => Equiv.refl K) tag ref u -
      KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram typeOf s (fun a => if a = r then τ else Equiv.refl K) tag ref u =
    if typeOf tag = u then 1 else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[AffineCellCounts] KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours.eq_1
KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours.eq_1.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {C : Type u_3}
  [Field K] [DecidableEq K] (s : G → K) (σ : G → Equiv.Perm K) (tag : G → C) (ref : C) (p : K × K) (i : G) :
  KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s σ tag ref p i =
    if (σ i) (KqR75.Transversal.point (s i) p) = 0 then tag i else ref
Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

[AffineCellCounts] KqR75.AffineCellCounts.pair_colour_sum
KqR75.AffineCellCounts.pair_colour_sum.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {C : Type u_3}
  [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] [Fintype G] [DecidableEq G] [DecidableEq C] (s : G → K)
  (tag : G → C) (ref : C) (i j : G) (hij : s i ≠ s j) (σ : G → Equiv.Perm K) (f : C → C → ℤ) :
  ∑ p,
      f (KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s σ tag ref p i)
        (KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s σ tag ref p j) =
    ∑ p,
      f (KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s (fun x => Equiv.refl K) tag ref p i)
        (KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s (fun x => Equiv.refl K) tag ref p j)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[AffineCellCounts] KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram.eq_1
KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram.eq_1.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {K : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {C : Type u_3}
  {T : Type u_4} [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] [DecidableEq T] (typeOf : (G → C) → T) (s : G → K)
  (σ : G → Equiv.Perm K) (tag : G → C) (ref : C) (u : T) :
  KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram typeOf s σ tag ref u =
    ∑ p, if typeOf (KqR75.AffineCellCounts.lineColours s σ tag ref p) = u then 1 else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankBudget] KqR75.BankCapacity.capacity_rounding
KqR75.BankCapacity.capacity_rounding {n : ℕ} {B : ℤ} {D z : ℝ} (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hB : 0 ≤ B) (hD : 0 ≤ D)
  (hz : z ≤ 2) :
  have X := ↑n ^ (2 - z);
  have ε := ⌈D * X⌉₊;
  have M := B.toNat * ε;
  B * ↑ε ≤ ↑M ∧ ↑M ≤ ↑B * (D + 1) * X ∧ ∀ (a : ℤ), |↑a| ≤ D * X → |a| ≤ ↑ε
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankBudget] KqR75.BankCapacity.block_card_of_marking
KqR75.BankCapacity.block_card_of_marking {n q : ℕ} (P : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))
  (X : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))
  (hpack : ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)), {b ∈ P | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card ≤ 1)
  (hmark : ∀ b ∈ P, (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ∩ X).Nonempty) : P.card ≤ X.card
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankBudget] KqR75.BankCapacity.edge_card_of_degree
KqR75.BankCapacity.edge_card_of_degree {n : ℕ} (X : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (D : ℝ)
  (h : ∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ D) : ↑X.card ≤ ↑n * D / 2
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankCapacity] KqR75.BankCapacity.bounded_integer_correction
KqR75.BankCapacity.bounded_integer_correction.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (s : Fin q → K)
  (hs : Function.Injective s) (ref : C) :
  ∃ tag r B,
    (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType (tag j) = ↑j) ∧
      0 ≤ B ∧
        ∀ (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ) (D : ℤ),
          (∀ (i : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), |w ↑i| ≤ D) →
            (∀ (i j : C), ∑ v, w v * ↑(KqR75.SlotPairMargins.orderedCount v i j) = 0) →
              ∃ a,
                (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), |a j| ≤ B * D) ∧
                  ∀ (v : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                    w v = ∑ k, a k * KqR75.SlotPairMargins.cellDifference s ref (tag k) (r k) v
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankDefect] KqR75.BankCapacity.defect_in_kernel
KqR75.BankCapacity.defect_in_kernel.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (colour : Fin n → C) (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), 2 ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i)
  (P : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ)
  (hdecomp :
    ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)), {b ∈ P | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card = 1)
  (hmargin :
    ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
        ∑ f,
          if KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑f = KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e then 1
          else 0)
  (i j : C) :
  ∑ t,
      (m t - KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour P t) * ↑(KqR75.SlotPairMargins.orderedCount t i j) =
    0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankDesign] KqR75.PhysicalBank.correct_design_with_bank
KqR75.PhysicalBank.correct_design_with_bank.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (s : Fin q → K)
  (hs : Function.Injective s) (ref : C) :
  ∃ tag r B,
    (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType (tag j) = ↑j) ∧
      0 ≤ B ∧
        ∀ {n M : ℕ} (colour : Fin n → C) (f : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M → Fin q × K ↪ Fin n),
          (∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (i : Fin q) (x : K),
              colour ((f j) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag j.1 i else ref) →
            (∀ (i j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M),
                i ≠ j → (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) →
              (∀ (i : C), 2 ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i) →
                ∀ (Q : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))
                  (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ) (ε : ℤ),
                  B * ε ≤ ↑M →
                    Disjoint (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks hq r s hs f) Q →
                      (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                          KqR75.PhysicalBank.edgeMultiplicity
                              (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks hq r s hs f ∪ Q) e =
                            1) →
                        (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                            KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
                              ∑ g,
                                if
                                    KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑g =
                                      KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e then
                                  1
                                else 0) →
                          (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                              |m t -
                                    KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour
                                      (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks hq r s hs f ∪ Q) t| ≤
                                ε) →
                            ∃ P,
                              Disjoint P Q ∧
                                (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                                    KqR75.PhysicalBank.edgeMultiplicity P e =
                                      KqR75.PhysicalBank.edgeMultiplicity
                                        (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks hq r s hs f) e) ∧
                                  (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                                      KqR75.PhysicalBank.edgeMultiplicity (P ∪ Q) e = 1) ∧
                                    ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                      KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour (P ∪ Q) t = m t
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankHorizon] KqR75.BankCapacity.prefix_histogram_bound
KqR75.BankCapacity.prefix_histogram_bound.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (hJ : 0 < J.card)
  (colour : Fin n → C) (w : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ)
  (hw : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), 0 ≤ w b)
  (hload : (KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w = fun x => 1) (a z D : ℝ) (ha : 0 ≤ a)
  (hz : z ≤ 2) (haz : z ≤ a) (h : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)))
  (hv : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) J h)
  (hh : h.length = KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon (↑J.card / ↑(q.choose 2)) n a)
  (hcount :
    ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
      |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour t) h) -
            ↑h.length *
              KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q)
                (KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J w) J (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour t) []| ≤
        D * ↑n ^ (2 - z))
  (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q) :
  |↑(KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h) t) -
        KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w t| ≤
    (D + 2) * ↑n ^ (2 - z)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankPlacement] KqR75.BankPlacement.place_indexed_bank
KqR75.BankPlacement.place_indexed_bank.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {C : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} [DecidableEq V]
  [Fintype V] {I : Type u_3} {S : Type u_4} [Fintype I] [Fintype S] (colour : I → S → C)
  (classes : C → Finset V) (forbidden : V → Finset V) (degree cap budget : ℕ) (hcap : 0 < cap)
  (hforbid : ∀ (v : V), (forbidden v).card ≤ degree) (hsym : ∀ (a b : V), a ∈ forbidden b ↔ b ∈ forbidden a)
  (htotal : Fintype.card I * Fintype.card S ≤ budget * cap)
  (hspace : ∀ (c : C), budget + Fintype.card S * (degree + Fintype.card S * cap + 1) < (classes c).card) :
  ∃ f,
    (∀ (i : I) (x : S), (f i) x ∈ classes (colour i x)) ∧
      (∀ (i : I) (x y : S), x ≠ y → (f i) x ∉ forbidden ((f i) y)) ∧
        (∀ (i j : I), i ≠ j → (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) ∧
          ∀ (v : V),
            (Finset.univ.biUnion fun i =>
                  if v ∈ Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ then Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ else ∅).card ≤
              Fintype.card S * cap
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankPlacement] KqR75.BankPlacement.usedNeighbors.eq_def

  (x✝ : List (Fin m ↪ V)) (x✝¹ : V) :
  KqR75.BankPlacement.usedNeighbors x✝ x✝¹ =
    match x✝, x✝¹ with
    | [], x => ∅
    | f :: cells, v =>
      (if v ∈ KqR75.BankPlacement.cellVertices f then KqR75.BankPlacement.cellVertices f else ∅) ∪
        KqR75.BankPlacement.usedNeighbors cells v

[BankPlacement] KqR75.BankPlacement.place_bank
KqR75.BankPlacement.place_bank.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} [DecidableEq V] {m : ℕ} [Fintype V]
  (requests : List (Fin m → C)) (classes : C → Finset V) (forbidden : V → Finset V) (degree cap budget : ℕ)
  (hcap : 0 < cap) (hforbid : ∀ (v : V), (forbidden v).card ≤ degree)
  (hsym : ∀ (a b : V), a ∈ forbidden b ↔ b ∈ forbidden a) (htotal : requests.length * m ≤ budget * cap)
  (hspace : ∀ (c : C), budget + m * (degree + m * cap + 1) < (classes c).card) :
  ∃ cells,
    List.Forall₂
        (fun colour f =>
          (∀ (i : Fin m), f i ∈ classes (colour i)) ∧ ∀ (i j : Fin m), i ≠ j → f i ∉ forbidden (f j))
        requests cells ∧
      List.Pairwise
          (fun f g => (KqR75.BankPlacement.cellVertices f ∩ KqR75.BankPlacement.cellVertices g).card ≤ 1)
          cells ∧
        (∀ (v : V), KqR75.BankPlacement.useCount cells v ≤ cap) ∧
          ∀ (v : V), (KqR75.BankPlacement.usedNeighbors cells v).card ≤ m * cap
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankReserve] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_basal_outside_reserve
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_basal_outside_reserve.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2}
  [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] (q : ℕ) (hq : 2 ≤ q) (ref : C)
  (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s) {ρ z B D : ℝ} (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hz : 0 < z) (hz₁ : z ≤ 1)
  (hB : 0 ≤ B) :
  ∃ G,
    0 < G ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (M degree : ℕ),
          ↑M ≤ B * ↑n ^ (2 - z) →
            ↑degree ≤ D * ↑n ^ (1 - z) →
              ∀ (tag : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q → C)
                (r : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q) (colour : Fin n → C)
                (ω : Sym2 (Fin n) → Bool),
                (∀ (c : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c)) →
                  (∀ (v : Fin n),
                      (KqR75.ReserveCuts.common (KqR75.ReserveRandom.adj ω) Finset.univ {v}).card ≤
                        degree) →
                    ∃ f F,
                      (∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (i : Fin q) (x : K),
                          colour ((f j) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag j.1 i else ref) ∧
                        (∀ (i j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M),
                            i ≠ j →
                              (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) ∧
                          KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks hq r s hs f) F ∧
                            Disjoint (KqR75.PhysicalReserve.edges ω) F ∧
                              ∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ G * ↑n ^ (1 - z)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankReserve] _private.BankReserve.0.KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_basal_outside_reserve._simp_1_1

[BankScales] KqR75.BankScales.eventually_place_copies
KqR75.BankScales.eventually_place_copies.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] (q : ℕ) (ref : C) {ρ z B D : ℝ} (hρ : 0 < ρ)
  (hz : 0 < z) (hz₁ : z ≤ 1) (hB : 0 ≤ B) :
  ∃ G,
    0 < G ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (M degree : ℕ),
          ↑M ≤ B * ↑n ^ (2 - z) →
            ↑degree ≤ D * ↑n ^ (1 - z) →
              ∀ (tag : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q → C) (colour : Fin n → C)
                (forbidden : Fin n → Finset (Fin n)),
                (∀ (c : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c)) →
                  (∀ (v : Fin n), (forbidden v).card ≤ degree) →
                    (∀ (a b : Fin n), a ∈ forbidden b ↔ b ∈ forbidden a) →
                      ∃ f,
                        (∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (i : Fin q) (x : K),
                            colour ((f j) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag j.1 i else ref) ∧
                          (∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (x y : Fin q × K),
                              x ≠ y → (f j) x ∉ forbidden ((f j) y)) ∧
                            (∀ (i j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M),
                                i ≠ j →
                                  (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) ∧
                              ∀ (v : Fin n),
                                ↑(Finset.univ.biUnion fun j =>
                                        if v ∈ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ then
                                          Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ
                                        else ∅).card ≤
                                  G * ↑n ^ (1 - z)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankScales] KqR75.BankScales.eventually_parameters
KqR75.BankScales.eventually_parameters (slots : ℕ) {ρ z B D : ℝ} (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hz : 0 < z) (hz₁ : z ≤ 1)
  (hB : 0 ≤ B) :
  ∃ G,
    0 < G ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (cells degree : ℕ),
          ↑cells ≤ B * ↑n ^ (2 - z) →
            ↑degree ≤ D * ↑n ^ (1 - z) →
              ∃ cap budget,
                0 < cap ∧
                  cells * slots ≤ budget * cap ∧
                    ↑(slots * cap) ≤ G * ↑n ^ (1 - z) ∧
                      ↑(budget + slots * (degree + slots * cap + 1)) < ρ * ↑n
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BankSwitch] KqR75.PhysicalBank.switch_whole_bank
KqR75.PhysicalBank.switch_whole_bank.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {n q M : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (ref : C)
  (tag : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q → C) (r : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q)
  (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (f : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M → Fin q × K ↪ Fin n)
  (hcolour :
    ∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (i : Fin q) (x : K),
      colour ((f j) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag j.1 i else ref)
  (hsep :
    ∀ (i j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M),
      i ≠ j → (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1)
  (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ) (a : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → ℤ)
  (ha : ∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), |a j| ≤ ↑M)
  (hw :
    ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
      w t = ∑ j, a j * KqR75.SlotPairMargins.cellDifference s ref (tag j) (r j) t) :
  ∃ P,
    (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
        {b ∈ P | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card =
          {b ∈ KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks hq r s hs f | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card) ∧
      ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
        ↑{b ∈ P | KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour b = t}.card -
            ↑{b ∈ KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks hq r s hs f |
                  KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour b = t}.card =
          w t
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[BasalBank] KqR75.BasalBank.packing_and_degree
KqR75.BasalBank.packing_and_degree.{u_1, u_2} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] [Fintype K] {I : Type u_2}
  (cells : Finset I) {n q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (f : I → Fin q × K ↪ Fin n) (s : Fin q → K)
  (hs : Function.Injective s) (σ : I → Fin q → Equiv.Perm K)
  (hsep :
    ∀ i ∈ cells,
      ∀ j ∈ cells, i ≠ j → (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) :
  have P := cells.biUnion fun j => KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.blocks hq (f j) s hs (σ j);
  have F := {e | ∃ b ∈ P, e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b};
  have J := Fᶜ;
  (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      ↑{b ∈ P | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card = if e ∈ J then 0 else 1) ∧
    ∀ (v : Fin n),
      ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤
        ↑(cells.biUnion fun j =>
              if v ∈ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ then Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ else ∅).card
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_designFibre_bilateral_of_omni._proof_1_1✝ (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q)

[Bilateral] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_designFibre_bilateral_of_omni
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_designFibre_bilateral_of_omni.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] [Nonempty C] (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ρ κ : ℝ) (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hκ : 0 < κ)
  (homni : KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.PhysicalOmniPlacement q (KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q)) :
  ∃ lowerErr upperErr,
    0 ≤ lowerErr ∧
      0 ≤ upperErr ∧
        ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
          ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ),
            (∀ (c : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c)) →
              (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                  KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
                    ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliques colour Finset.univ
                        (KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e))) →
                (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                    κ * (↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑(q.choose 2)) ≤ ↑(m t)) →
                  q.choose 2 ∣ n.choose 2 →
                    q - 1 ∣ n - 1 →
                      have r := q.choose 2;
                      have z := KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q;
                      have s := ↑(r - 1);
                      have B := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r;
                      have N := fun t =>
                        ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) t).card;
                      have K := ∑ t, ↑(m t) / B * Real.log (↑(m t) / B / (N t / ↑(n.choose q)));
                      have main := B * (Real.log ↑((n - 2).choose (q - 2)) - s - K);
                      0 < (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ∧
                        main - lowerErr * (↑n ^ (2 - z) * Real.log ↑n) ≤
                            Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ∧
                          Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ≤
                            main + upperErr * ↑n ^ (2 - 1 / s)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CliqueCodegrees] KqR75.CliqueCodegrees.codegree_le_complete

  (e f : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) :

  (colour : Fin n → C) (hq : 2 ≤ q) (hn : 2 * q ≤ n)

  (hm : ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q), a * ↑n ^ 2 ≤ m t)
  (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q) :
  a / (2 ^ (q - 2) * ↑(q - 2).factorial) * (↑((n - 2).choose (q - 2)))⁻¹ ≤
    KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) m b
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

            ↑(KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour basal t) -

[CompletionWithCapacity] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.complete_with_capacity
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.complete_with_capacity.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {q : ℕ} (hq : 3 ≤ q) (s : Fin q → K)
  (hs : Function.Injective s) (ref : C) :
  ∃ tag r B,
    (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType (tag j) = ↑j) ∧
      0 ≤ B ∧
        ∀ {n M : ℕ},
          1 ≤ n →
            ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (f : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M → Fin q × K ↪ Fin n),
              (∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (i : Fin q) (x : K),
                  colour ((f j) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag j.1 i else ref) →
                (∀ (i j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M),
                    i ≠ j → (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) →
                  (∀ (i : C), 2 ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i) →
                    ∀ (A X F : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                      Disjoint A X →
                        Disjoint A F →
                          Disjoint X F →
                            KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs
                                (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks
                                  (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.complete_with_capacity._proof_2 hq) r s hs f)
                                F →
                              (∀ S ⊆ X,
                                  KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.Divisible q S →
                                    ∃ P, KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P (A ∪ S)) →
                                KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.Divisible q Finset.univ →
                                  ∀ (G U z E₁ E₂ : ℝ),
                                    z ≤ 2 →
                                      0 ≤ E₁ + E₂ + G + U →
                                        B.toNat * ⌈(E₁ + E₂ + G + U) * ↑n ^ (2 - z)⌉₊ ≤ M →
                                          (∀ (v : Fin n),
                                              ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ G * ↑n ^ (1 - z)) →
                                            (∀ (v : Fin n),
                                                ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤
                                                  U * ↑n ^ (1 - z)) →
                                              ∀ (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ),
                                                (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                                                    KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour
                                                        (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
                                                      ∑ g,
                                                        if
                                                            KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑g =
                                                              KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e then
                                                          1
                                                        else 0) →
                                                  ∀ (P₀ : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)),
                                                    KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P₀ A →
                                                      ∀
                                                        (h :
                                                          List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)))
                                                        (repaired :
                                                          KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ),
                                                        KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid
                                                            (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) (A ∪ X ∪ F)ᶜ
                                                            h →
                                                          (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                                              |repaired t -
                                                                    ↑(KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram
                                                                        colour
                                                                        (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h)
                                                                        t)| ≤
                                                                E₂ * ↑n ^ (2 - z)) →
                                                            (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                                                |↑(m t) -
                                                                        ↑(KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram
                                                                            colour
                                                                            (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks
                                                                                (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.complete_with_capacity._proof_4
                                                                                  hq t)
                                                                                r s hs f ∪
                                                                              P₀)
                                                                            t) -
                                                                      repaired t| ≤
                                                                  E₁ * ↑n ^ (2 - z)) →
                                                              (∃ PC,
                                                                  ∃ Y ⊆ X,
                                                                    KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs PC
                                                                        (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining
                                                                            (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q)
                                                                            (A ∪ X ∪ F)ᶜ h ∪
                                                                          Y) ∧
                                                                      ∀ b ∈ PC,
                                                                        (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ∩
                                                                            X).Nonempty) →
                                                                ∃ P,
                                                                  KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P
                                                                      Finset.univ ∧
                                                                    (∀
                                                                        (t :
                                                                          KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile
                                                                            C q),
                                                                        KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram
                                                                            colour P t =
                                                                          m t) ∧
                                                                      {b ∈ P |
                                                                          KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ⊆
                                                                            (A ∪ X ∪ F)ᶜ} =
                                                                        KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CorrectedCompletion] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.corrected_completion
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.corrected_completion.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (s : Fin q → K)
  (hs : Function.Injective s) (ref : C) :
  ∃ tag r B,
    (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType (tag j) = ↑j) ∧
      0 ≤ B ∧
        ∀ {n M : ℕ} (colour : Fin n → C) (f : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M → Fin q × K ↪ Fin n),
          (∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (i : Fin q) (x : K),
              colour ((f j) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag j.1 i else ref) →
            (∀ (i j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M),
                i ≠ j → (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) →
              (∀ (i : C), 2 ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i) →
                ∀ (A X F Y : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                  Disjoint A X →
                    Disjoint A F →
                      Disjoint X F →
                        Y ⊆ X →
                          ∀ (h : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)))
                            (PC PA : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)),
                            KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) (A ∪ X ∪ F)ᶜ h →
                              KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs PC
                                  (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q)
                                      (A ∪ X ∪ F)ᶜ h ∪
                                    Y) →
                                (∀ b ∈ PC, (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ∩ X).Nonempty) →
                                  KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs PA (A ∪ X \ Y) →
                                    KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs
                                        (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks hq r s hs f) F →
                                      ∀ (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ) (ε : ℤ),
                                        B * ε ≤ ↑M →
                                          (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                                              KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
                                                ∑ g,
                                                  if
                                                      KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑g =
                                                        KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e then
                                                    1
                                                  else 0) →
                                            (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                                |m t -
                                                      KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour
                                                        (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks hq r s hs f ∪
                                                          (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h ∪ PC ∪ PA))
                                                        t| ≤
                                                  ε) →
                                              ∃ P,
                                                KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P Finset.univ ∧
                                                  (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                                      KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour P t =
                                                        m t) ∧
                                                    {b ∈ P |
                                                        KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ⊆ (A ∪ X ∪ F)ᶜ} =
                                                      KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverAssembly] KqR75.CoverDown.cover_two_phases
KqR75.CoverDown.cover_two_phases.{u_1} {V : Type u_1} [DecidableEq V] (R : V → V → Prop) [DecidableRel R]
  [Std.Symm R] (S : Finset V) (L : Finset (Finset V)) (q dL dY dP : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q)
  (hd : Disjoint L (KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S)) (hcap : dY + (q - 2) * dL ≤ dP)
  (chooseStar :
    ∀ (J Y : Finset (Finset V)),
      J ⊆ L →
        Y ⊆ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S →
          Y.card ≤ q.choose 2 * (L.card - J.card) →
            (∀ (v : V), KqR75.CoverDown.degree Y v ≤ dY) →
              ∀ (u : V),
                dL < KqR75.CoverDown.degree J u →
                  ∃ C ⊆ S,
                    (↑C).Pairwise R ∧
                      u ∉ C ∧
                        C.card = q - 1 ∧
                          (∀ w ∈ C, {u, w} ∈ J ∧ KqR75.CoverDown.degree Y w + (q - 2) ≤ dY) ∧
                            Disjoint (Finset.powersetCard 2 C) Y)
  (choosePair :
    ∀ (J Y : Finset (Finset V)),
      J ⊆ L →
        Y ⊆ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S →
          Y.card ≤ q.choose 2 * (L.card - J.card) →
            (∀ (v : V), KqR75.CoverDown.potential q J Y v ≤ dP) →
              ∀ e ∈ J,
                ∃ C,
                  C.card = q ∧
                    e ∈ Finset.powersetCard 2 C ∧
                      (Finset.powersetCard 2 C).erase e ⊆ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S \ Y ∧
                        (Finset.powersetCard 2 C ∩ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S).Nonempty ∧
                          ∀ w ∈ C, w ∉ e → KqR75.CoverDown.potential q J Y w + (q - 1) ≤ dP) :
  ∃ P Y, KqR75.CoverDown.Cover q L (KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S) P ∅ Y ∧ Y.card ≤ q.choose 2 * L.card
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverChoices] KqR75.CoverDown.exists_clean_extension_of_bounds

  [DecidableRel R] (S H Z : Finset V) (Y : Finset (Finset V)) (k : ℕ) (bv be : ℝ) (hv₀ : 0 ≤ bv)
  (he₀ : 0 ≤ be) (hv : ∀ v ∈ Z, ↑{C ∈ KqR75.ReserveCuts.extensions R S H k | v ∈ C}.card ≤ bv)
  (he : ∀ e ∈ Y, ↑{x ∈ KqR75.ReserveCuts.extensions R S H k | e ⊆ x}.card ≤ be)
  (hroom : ↑Z.card * bv + ↑Y.card * be < ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.extensions R S H k).card) :

[CoverChoices] KqR75.CoverDown.extension_fibre_density
KqR75.CoverDown.extension_fibre_density.{u_1} {V : Type u_1} [DecidableEq V] (R : V → V → Prop)
  [DecidableRel R] (hirr : ∀ (v : V), ¬R v v) (S : Finset V) (p : ℝ) (hp : 0 ≤ p) (d : ℕ)
  (hcommon : ∀ H ⊆ S, H.card < d → ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.common R S H).card ≤ 2 * ↑S.card * p ^ H.card)
  (H A : Finset V) (k : ℕ) (hH : H ⊆ S) (hk : H.card + k ≤ d) :
  ↑{x ∈ KqR75.ReserveCuts.extensions R S H k | A ⊆ x}.card ≤
    (2 * ↑S.card) ^ (k - A.card) * p ^ ((H.card + A.card) * (k - A.card) + (k - A.card).choose 2)

[CoverChoices] KqR75.CoverDown.exists_clean_extension

  [DecidableRel R] (S H Z : Finset V) (Y : Finset (Finset V)) (k : ℕ) (bf : V → ℕ) (be : Finset V → ℕ)

  (he : ∀ e ∈ Y, {x ∈ KqR75.ReserveCuts.extensions R S H k | e ⊆ x}.card ≤ be e)

[CoverComplete] _private.CoverComplete.0.KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover._proof_1_2

[CoverComplete] KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover
KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (z M : ℝ) (hz : 0 < z) (hM : 0 ≤ M)
  (hgap : (2 * ↑q + 1) * z < 1) :
  ∃ K₀,
    1 ≤ K₀ ∧
      ∀ K ≥ K₀,
        ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
          ∃ p ω,
            ↑p = K * ↑n ^ (-z) ∧
              KqR75.ReserveRandom.RootGood p ω (q - 1) ∧
                ∀ (L : Finset (Finset (Fin n))),
                  (∀ e ∈ L, e.card = 2) →
                    ↑L.card ≤ M * ↑n ^ (2 - (2 * ↑q - 1) * z) →
                      Disjoint L (KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges (KqR75.ReserveRandom.adj ω) Finset.univ) →
                        ∃ P Y,
                          KqR75.CoverDown.Cover q L
                              (KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges (KqR75.ReserveRandom.adj ω) Finset.univ) P ∅ Y ∧
                            Y.card ≤ q.choose 2 * L.card
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverDown] KqR75.CoverDown.pair_step_potential
KqR75.CoverDown.pair_step_potential.{u_1} {V : Type u_1} [DecidableEq V] {L Y : Finset (Finset V)}
  (C e : Finset V) (he : e ∈ Finset.powersetCard 2 C) (heL : e ∈ L)
  (hother : Disjoint ((Finset.powersetCard 2 C).erase e) L)
  (hunused : Disjoint Y ((Finset.powersetCard 2 C).erase e)) (v : V) :
  have q := C.card;
  have out := KqR75.CoverDown.take L Y (Finset.powersetCard 2 C);
  KqR75.CoverDown.degree out.2 v + (q - 2) * KqR75.CoverDown.degree out.1 v =
    KqR75.CoverDown.degree Y v + (q - 2) * KqR75.CoverDown.degree L v + if v ∈ C ∧ v ∉ e then q - 1 else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverDown] KqR75.CoverDown.star_step

  (S : Finset V) (hu : u ∉ S) (hstar : KqR75.CoverDown.star u S ⊆ L)
  (hinside : Disjoint (Finset.powersetCard 2 S) L) (hunused : Disjoint Y (Finset.powersetCard 2 S)) :
  have out := KqR75.CoverDown.take L Y (Finset.powersetCard 2 (Insert.insert u S));
  out.1.card + S.card = L.card ∧
    KqR75.CoverDown.degree out.1 u + S.card = KqR75.CoverDown.degree L u ∧
      ∀ (v : V),
        KqR75.CoverDown.degree out.2 v = KqR75.CoverDown.degree Y v + if v ∈ S then S.card - 1 else 0

[CoverIteration] KqR75.CoverDown.run_phase
KqR75.CoverDown.run_phase.{u_1} {V : Type u_1} [DecidableEq V] {q : ℕ} {L X P R Y : Finset (Finset V)}
  (hd : Disjoint L X) (done : Finset (Finset V) → Prop)
  (guard : Finset (Finset V) → Finset (Finset V) → Prop) (hP : KqR75.CoverDown.Cover q L X P R Y)
  (hg : guard R Y) (hY : Y.card ≤ q.choose 2 * (L.card - R.card))
  (select :
    ∀ (R' Y' : Finset (Finset V)),
      R' ⊆ L →
        Y' ⊆ X →
          guard R' Y' →
            Y'.card ≤ q.choose 2 * (L.card - R'.card) →
              ¬done R' →
                ∃ C,
                  C.card = q ∧
                    Finset.powersetCard 2 C ⊆ R' ∪ X \ Y' ∧
                      (Finset.powersetCard 2 C ∩ X).Nonempty ∧
                        (R' ∩ Finset.powersetCard 2 C).Nonempty ∧
                          have out := KqR75.CoverDown.take R' Y' (Finset.powersetCard 2 C);
                          guard out.1 out.2) :
  ∃ P' R' Y',
    P ⊆ P' ∧
      KqR75.CoverDown.Cover q L X P' R' Y' ∧
        done R' ∧ guard R' Y' ∧ Y'.card ≤ q.choose 2 * (L.card - R'.card)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverIteration] _private.CoverIteration.0.KqR75.CoverDown.run_phase._proof_1_1

  (m : ℕ) {R Y : Finset (Finset V)} (hm : R.card = m) (C : Finset V)

[CoverPairSelector] KqR75.CoverDown.select_pair
KqR75.CoverDown.select_pair.{u_1} {V : Type u_1} [Fintype V] [DecidableEq V] (R : V → V → Prop)
  [DecidableRel R] [Std.Symm R] (hirr : ∀ (v : V), ¬R v v) (p : ℝ) (hp : 0 ≤ p) (q cap zmax B M : ℕ)
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hcap : q - 1 ≤ cap)
  (hcommon :
    ∀ (H : Finset V),
      H.card < q → ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.common R Finset.univ H).card ≤ 2 * ↑(Fintype.card V) * p ^ H.card)
  (hZ : 2 * (B + (q - 2) * M) ≤ zmax * (cap - (q - 1) + 1))
  (hcount :
    ∀ (u v : V),
      u ≠ v →
        (↑(zmax + 2 * cap) * ((2 * ↑(Fintype.card V)) ^ (q - 3) * p ^ (3 * (q - 3) + (q - 3).choose 2)) +
            ↑B *
              if 4 ≤ q then (2 * ↑(Fintype.card V)) ^ (q - 4) * p ^ (4 * (q - 4) + (q - 4).choose 2)
              else 0) <
          ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.rootedExtensions R Finset.univ u v (q - 2)).card)
  (J Y : Finset (Finset V)) (hY : ∀ e ∈ Y, e.card = 2) (hJ : ∀ e ∈ J, e.card = 2) (hJcard : J.card ≤ M)
  (hYcard : Y.card ≤ B) (hP : ∀ (w : V), KqR75.CoverDown.potential q J Y w ≤ cap) (e : Finset V)
  (he : e ∈ J) :
  ∃ D,
    D.card = q ∧
      e ∈ Finset.powersetCard 2 D ∧
        (Finset.powersetCard 2 D).erase e ⊆ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R Finset.univ \ Y ∧
          (Finset.powersetCard 2 D ∩ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R Finset.univ).Nonempty ∧
            ∀ w ∈ D, w ∉ e → KqR75.CoverDown.potential q J Y w + (q - 1) ≤ cap
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverRatios] _private.CoverRatios.0.KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_ratios._proof_1_4
KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_ratios._proof_1_4✝ (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) : ¬2 ≤ q - 1 → False
Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

[CoverRatios] KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_ratios
KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_ratios (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (z : ℝ) (hz : 0 < z)
  (Mstar Mlink Msat Mused cstar cpair : ℝ) (hcstar : 0 < cstar) (hcpair : 0 < cpair) :
  ∃ K₀,
    1 ≤ K₀ ∧
      ∀ (K : ℝ),
        K₀ ≤ K →
          ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
            have x := ↑n;
            have p := K * x ^ (-z);
            have a := (2 * ↑q - 1) * z;
            have s := ↑(q - 1) * (1 - 2 * z);
            have l := ↑(q - 2);
            have b := (q - 1).choose 2;
            have r := q.choose 2 - 1;
            Mstar * x ^ (↑(q - 3) + 2 - a) * p ^ (b - 1) < cstar * x ^ s * p ^ b ∧
              Mlink * x ^ (↑(q - 3) + 1 - 2 * z) * p ^ (r - 2) < cpair * x ^ l * p ^ r ∧
                Msat * x ^ (↑(q - 3) + 1 - a + 2 * z) * p ^ (r - 2) < cpair * x ^ l * p ^ r ∧
                  (4 ≤ q → Mused * x ^ (↑(q - 4) + 2 - a) * p ^ (r - 5) < cpair * x ^ l * p ^ r)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  ¬5 ≤ q.choose 2 - 1 → False
Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

[CoverScales] KqR75.CoverDown.obstruction_ratio
KqR75.CoverDown.obstruction_ratio {x K z : ℝ} (hx : 0 < x) (hK : 0 < K) (s u : ℝ) {b j : ℕ} (hj : j ≤ b) :
  have p := K * x ^ (-z);
  x ^ s * p ^ (b - j) = x ^ u * p ^ b * (x ^ (s - u + z * ↑j) / K ^ j)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverScales] KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_small_obstruction

  (hj : j ≤ b) (hgap : s - u + z * ↑j < 0) :
  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    have p := K * ↑n ^ (-z);
    M * ↑n ^ s * p ^ (b - j) < c * ↑n ^ u * p ^ b

[CoverSelectors] KqR75.CoverDown.select_star
KqR75.CoverDown.select_star.{u_1} {V : Type u_1} [DecidableEq V] [Fintype V] (R : V → V → Prop)
  [DecidableRel R] (hirr : ∀ (v : V), ¬R v v) (p : ℝ) (hp : 0 ≤ p) (q dL dY m zmax B : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q)
  (hcap : q - 2 ≤ dY)

    ∀ (H : Finset V),
      H.card < q → ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.common R Finset.univ H).card ≤ 2 * ↑(Fintype.card V) * p ^ H.card)

  (hcount :
    ∀ (S : Finset V),
      m ≤ S.card →
        ↑B * ((2 * ↑(Fintype.card V)) ^ (q - 3) * p ^ (2 * (q - 3) + (q - 3).choose 2)) <
          ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.cliquesIn R S (q - 1)).card)
  (J Y : Finset (Finset V)) (hJ : ∀ e ∈ J, e.card = 2) (hY : ∀ e ∈ Y, e.card = 2) (hYcard : Y.card ≤ B)
  (u : V) (hu : dL < KqR75.CoverDown.degree J u) :
  ∃ C,
    (↑C).Pairwise R ∧
      u ∉ C ∧
        C.card = q - 1 ∧
          (∀ w ∈ C, {u, w} ∈ J ∧ KqR75.CoverDown.degree Y w + (q - 2) ≤ dY) ∧
            Disjoint (Finset.powersetCard 2 C) Y
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverSteps] KqR75.CoverDown.star_extension
KqR75.CoverDown.star_extension.{u_1} {V : Type u_1} [DecidableEq V] (R : V → V → Prop) [DecidableRel R]
  (S C : Finset V) (u : V) (L Y : Finset (Finset V)) (hS : C ⊆ S) (hClique : (↑C).Pairwise R) (hu : u ∉ C)
  (hsize : 2 ≤ C.card) (hstar : ∀ w ∈ C, {u, w} ∈ L) (hclean : Disjoint (Finset.powersetCard 2 C) Y) :
  have B := Finset.powersetCard 2 (Insert.insert u C);
  B ⊆ L ∪ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S \ Y ∧
    (B ∩ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S).Nonempty ∧ (L ∩ B).Nonempty

[CoverSteps] KqR75.CoverDown.pair_extension
KqR75.CoverDown.pair_extension.{u_1} {V : Type u_1} [DecidableEq V] (R : V → V → Prop) [DecidableRel R]
  [Std.Symm R] (S C : Finset V) (u v : V) (Y : Finset (Finset V)) (hS : C ⊆ S) (hClique : (↑C).Pairwise R)
  (hu : u ∈ S) (hv : v ∈ S) (huv : u ≠ v) (huC : u ∉ C) (hvC : v ∉ C) (hsize : 0 < C.card)
  (hlinks : ∀ w ∈ C, R u w ∧ R v w) (hclean : Disjoint (Finset.powersetCard 2 C) Y)
  (hlinksClean : ∀ w ∈ C, {u, w} ∉ Y ∧ {v, w} ∉ Y) :
  have B := Finset.powersetCard 2 (Insert.insert u (Insert.insert v C));
  {u, v} ∈ B ∧
    B.erase {u, v} ⊆ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S \ Y ∧ (B ∩ KqR75.CoverDown.reserveEdges R S).Nonempty
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverThresholds] KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_thresholds
KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_thresholds (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (z c M : ℝ) (hz : 0 < z) (hc : 0 < c)
  (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hgap : (2 * ↑q + 1) * z < 1) :
  have a := (2 * ↑q - 1) * z;
  have β := 1 - 2 * z;
  have γ := 1 - a + 2 * z;
  have D := 1 + 4 * (↑(q.choose 2) + ↑(q - 2)) * M;
  have P := 2 + ↑(q - 2) * (2 * c + 1);
  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    ∀ (N : ℕ),
      ↑N ≤ M * ↑n ^ (2 - a) →
        have x := ↑n;
        have B := q.choose 2 * N;
        have dL := ⌈2 * c * x ^ β⌉₊;
        have dY := ⌈x ^ β⌉₊;
        have cap := dY + (q - 2) * dL;
        have m := ⌈c * x ^ β⌉₊;
        have zmax := ⌈D * x ^ γ⌉₊;
        q - 2 ≤ dY ∧
          q - 1 ≤ cap ∧
            m + zmax ≤ dL + 1 ∧
              2 * B ≤ zmax * (dY - (q - 2) + 1) ∧
                2 * (B + (q - 2) * N) ≤ zmax * (cap - (q - 1) + 1) ∧
                  ↑zmax ≤ (D + 1) * x ^ γ ∧ ↑cap ≤ P * x ^ β
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverThresholds] _private.CoverThresholds.0.KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_thresholds._proof_1_1
KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_thresholds._proof_1_1✝ (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (z c : ℝ) (n : ℕ)
  (hstep : q - 1 ≤ ⌈↑n ^ (1 - 2 * z)⌉₊) : ¬q - 2 ≤ ⌈↑n ^ (1 - 2 * z)⌉₊ → False
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CoverTransfer] KqR75.CoverDown.physical_completion
KqR75.CoverDown.physical_completion {n q : ℕ} {L X : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)}
  {P Y : Finset (Finset (Fin n))}
  (h : KqR75.CoverDown.Cover q (KqR75.CoverDown.vertexSets L) (KqR75.CoverDown.vertexSets X) P ∅ Y) :
  have out := KqR75.CoverDown.physicalSets q P;
  have Y' := KqR75.CoverDown.physicalSets 2 Y;
  Y' ⊆ X ∧
    Y'.card = Y.card ∧
      (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
          {C ∈ out | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q C}.card = if e ∈ L ∪ Y' then 1 else 0) ∧
        ∀ C ∈ out, (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q C ∩ X).Nonempty
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CumulativeTypeDrift] KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.physical_path_bound
KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.physical_path_bound.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (w : B), 0 ≤ μ w)
  (J : Finset E) (S : Finset B) (r T n : ℕ) (M β b c C A a z : ℝ) (hr : 2 ≤ r) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hc : 0 < c)
  (hC : 0 ≤ C) (hA : 0 ≤ A) (hMₗ : c * ↑n ^ 2 ≤ M) (hMᵤ : M ≤ C * ↑n ^ 2) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hz : 0 ≤ z)
  (hs : a * (4 * ↑r - 1) = 1 - 2 * z) (horizon : T ≤ KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a)
  (hsize : ∀ (w : B), (I w).card = r) (hJ : ↑J.card = ↑r * M)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) (hb : 0 ≤ b) (hβrate : β ≤ b / ↑n)
  (hcodeg : ∀ (e f : E), e ≠ f → KqR75.CodegreeLoss.codegree I μ e f ≤ β) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop)
  (hguard :
    ∀ (h : List (Option B)),
      guard h →
        KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M (↑n) β h ∧
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
                (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M) h ≤
              A * ↑n ∧
            2 * ↑r ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h)
  (h : List (Option B)) (hlen : h.length ≤ T) :
  have K := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard;
  |LatinonEntropy.pathSum
        (fun g x => ∑ x, K.transition g x * KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment I μ J S guard g x) h| ≤
    KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.driftConstant r c C A b * ↑n ^ (-z)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CumulativeTypeDrift] _private.CumulativeTypeDrift.0.KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment.match_1.eq_1
KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment.match_1.eq_1✝.{u_1, u_2} {B : Type u_1} (motive : Option B → Sort u_2)
  (h_1 : Unit → motive Option.none) (h_2 : (c : B) → motive (Option.some c)) :
  (match Option.none with
    | Option.none => h_1 ()
    | Option.some c => h_2 c) =
    h_1 ()
Axioms: []

[CumulativeTypeDrift] KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.sum_envelope
KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.sum_envelope (n T r : ℕ) (M c C A b a z : ℝ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hr : 2 ≤ r)
  (hc : 0 < c) (hC : 0 ≤ C) (hA : 0 ≤ A) (hb : 0 ≤ b) (hM : 0 < M) (hMᵤ : M ≤ C * ↑n ^ 2) (ha : 0 ≤ a)
  (hz : 0 ≤ z) (hs : a * (4 * ↑r - 1) = 1 - 2 * z) (hT : T ≤ KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a) :
  ∑ i ∈ Finset.range T, KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.envelope r M (↑n) c C A b i ≤
    KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.driftConstant r c C A b * ↑n ^ (-z)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CumulativeTypeDrift] KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.envelope.eq_1
KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.envelope.eq_1 (r : ℕ) (M n c C A b : ℝ) (i : ℕ) :
  KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.envelope r M n c C A b i =
    (2 / c) ^ 2 * A * (n ^ (-3) * ((M - ↑i) / M) ^ (-4 * ↑r)) +
          (2 / c) ^ 2 * √(↑r * C * A) * (n ^ (-5 / 2) * ((M - ↑i) / M) ^ (-2 * ↑r - 1 / 2)) +
        2 / c * ↑r ^ 2 * b * (n ^ (-3) * ((M - ↑i) / M) ^ (-↑r)) +
      (2 / c) ^ 2 * (2 * ↑r ^ 2) * (n ^ (-4) * ((M - ↑i) / M) ^ (-2 * ↑r))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[CumulativeTypeDrift] KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.driftConstant.eq_1
KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.driftConstant.eq_1 (r : ℕ) (c C A b : ℝ) :
  KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.driftConstant r c C A b =
    (2 / c) ^ 2 * A * (C / (4 * ↑r - 1)) + (2 / c) ^ 2 * √(↑r * C * A) * (C / (2 * ↑r - 1 / 2)) +
        2 / c * ↑r ^ 2 * b * (C / (↑r - 1)) +
      (2 / c) ^ 2 * (2 * ↑r ^ 2) * (C / (2 * ↑r - 1))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.envelope._proof_2 : (2 + 1).AtLeastTwo
Axioms: [propext]

[DesignFibre] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.log_designFibre_of_good_histories
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.log_designFibre_of_good_histories.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  {n q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ)
  (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))
  (K : LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))) (T : ℕ)
  (good : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) → Prop) (Q : ℝ)
  (hmass : 1 / 2 ≤ KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T good)
  (hgood :
    ∀ h ∈ LatinonEntropy.histories (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) T,
      good h →
        0 < LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h →
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) J h ∧
            Option.none ∉ h ∧
              (∃ P,
                  KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P Finset.univ ∧
                    (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                        KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour P t = m t) ∧
                      {b ∈ P | KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ⊆ J} = KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h) ∧
                Real.log (LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h) + Real.log ↑T.factorial ≤ Q) :
  0 < (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ∧
    -Q - Real.log 2 ≤ Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[DesignFibre] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre.congr_simp

  KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m = KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour✝ m✝

[DesignFibreMass] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre_type_score_as_entropy
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre_type_score_as_entropy.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  {n q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (hnq : q ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i)
  (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ) (P : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))
  (hP : P ∈ KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m) :
  have b := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑(q.choose 2);
  have N := fun t => ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) t).card;
  have K := ∑ t, ↑(m t) / b * Real.log (↑(m t) / b / (N t / ↑(n.choose q)));
  -∑ t, ↑(m t) * Real.log (↑(m t) / N t) = b * (Real.log ↑((n - 2).choose (q - 2)) - K)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[DesignProfileCounts] KqR75.ProfileLDP.total_eq_unit_fibre
KqR75.ProfileLDP.total_eq_unit_fibre {n q : ℕ} (hn : q ≤ n) (hq : 2 ≤ q)
  (had : KqR75.ProfileLDP.admissible q n) :
  have m := fun x => ↑(n.choose 2 / q.choose 2);
  KqR75.ProfileLDP.designs q n = KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre (fun x => ()) m ∧
    KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.ExactPairMargins (fun x => ()) m ∧
      ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile Unit q),
        KqR75.ProfileLDP.normalizedQuota n m t = 1 ∧ KqR75.ProfileLDP.finiteTypeLaw (fun x => ()) t = 1

[DesignProfileLDP] KqR75.ProfileLDP.designProfileLDP

  [Nonempty C] (colour : (n : ℕ) → Fin n → C) (p : C → ℝ) (hp : ∀ (c : C), 0 < p c)
  (hcolour :
    ∀ (c : C),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n => ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile (colour n) Finset.univ c) / ↑n)
        (KqR75.ProfileLDP.admissibleOrders q) (nhds (p c))) :
  have P := KqR75.ProfileLDP.multinomialTypeLaw p;
  (KqR75.ProfileLDP.CountLDP (KqR75.ProfileLDP.admissible q) (fun n => ↑(KqR75.ProfileLDP.designs q n).card)
      (fun n => KqR75.ProfileLDP.profileCount (colour n)) (fun n => KqR75.ProfileLDP.blockMass n q)
      (KqR75.ProfileLDP.feasible P) fun Q => KqR75.ProfileLDP.KL Q P) ∧
    (∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in KqR75.ProfileLDP.admissibleOrders q, 0 < (KqR75.ProfileLDP.designs q n).card) ∧
      ∀ (a : ℝ), IsCompact {Q | Q ∈ KqR75.ProfileLDP.feasible P ∧ KqR75.ProfileLDP.KL Q P ≤ a}
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[DesignProfileLDP] _private.DesignProfileLDP.0.KqR75.ProfileLDP.eventually_upper_profiles

  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (colour : (n : ℕ) → Fin n → C) {P : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ}
  (hP :

      (KqR75.ProfileLDP.admissibleOrders q) (nhds P))

[DesignProfileLDP] KqR75.ProfileLDP.designProfileLDP_probability
KqR75.ProfileLDP.designProfileLDP_probability.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {q : ℕ}
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) [Nonempty C] (colour : (n : ℕ) → Fin n → C) (p : C → ℝ) (hp : ∀ (c : C), 0 < p c)
  (hcolour :
    ∀ (c : C),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n => ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile (colour n) Finset.univ c) / ↑n)
        (KqR75.ProfileLDP.admissibleOrders q) (nhds (p c))) :
  have P := KqR75.ProfileLDP.multinomialTypeLaw p;
  (∀ (F : Set (KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ)),
      IsClosed F →
        ∀ (r : ℝ),
          (∀ Q ∈ F, Q ∈ KqR75.ProfileLDP.feasible P → r < KqR75.ProfileLDP.KL Q P) →
            ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in KqR75.ProfileLDP.admissibleOrders q,
              KqR75.ProfileLDP.profileCount (colour n) F / ↑(KqR75.ProfileLDP.designs q n).card ≤
                Real.exp (-(KqR75.ProfileLDP.blockMass n q * r))) ∧
    (∀ (G : Set (KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ)),
        IsOpen G →
          ∀ Q ∈ G,
            Q ∈ KqR75.ProfileLDP.feasible P →
              ∀ (ε : ℝ),
                0 < ε →
                  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in KqR75.ProfileLDP.admissibleOrders q,
                    Real.exp (-(KqR75.ProfileLDP.blockMass n q * (KqR75.ProfileLDP.KL Q P + ε))) ≤
                      KqR75.ProfileLDP.profileCount (colour n) G / ↑(KqR75.ProfileLDP.designs q n).card) ∧
      ∀ (a : ℝ), IsCompact {Q | Q ∈ KqR75.ProfileLDP.feasible P ∧ KqR75.ProfileLDP.KL Q P ≤ a}
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[DistinctTriples] KqR75.distinct_triple_sum
KqR75.distinct_triple_sum.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] (f g h : ι → ℝ) :
  (∑ i, ∑ j, ∑ k, if i ≠ j ∧ j ≠ k ∧ k ≠ i then f i * g j * h k else 0) =
    ((∑ i, f i) * ∑ j, g j) * ∑ k, h k - (∑ i, f i * g i) * ∑ k, h k - (∑ j, g j * h j) * ∑ i, f i -
        (∑ k, h k * f k) * ∑ j, g j +
      2 * ∑ i, f i * g i * h i
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[EnergyExit] KqR75.EnergyExit.first_exit_bound
KqR75.EnergyExit.first_exit_bound.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (r : ℕ) (M : ℝ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (hcard : ↑J.card = ↑r * M)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1) (n β b : ℝ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hβ : 0 ≤ β)
  (hb : 0 ≤ b) (hβrate : β ≤ b / n) (hcodeg : ∀ (e f : E), e ≠ f → KqR75.CodegreeLoss.codegree I μ e f ≤ β)
  (extra : List (Option B) → Prop) (T : ℕ) (u : ℝ) (hu : 0 < u) :
  have Y :=
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
      (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M);
  have K :=
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J fun h =>
      KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M n β h ∧ extra h ∧ Y h < u;
  KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T (KqR75.EnergyExit.crossed Y u) ≤
    (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J [] 1 + ↑T * (KqR75.PhysicalDrift.noiseConstant r b / n)) / u
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[GeneralJohnson] KqR75.GeneralJohnson.orbit_identities
KqR75.GeneralJohnson.orbit_identities (q n : ℝ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hn : q + 2 ≤ n) :
  KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u0 q n + 2 * (n - 2) * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n +
        (n - 2) * (n - 3) / 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n =
      1 ∧
    KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u0 q n +
                (n - 2) * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n +
              (n - 3) * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n +
            (n - 3) * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n +
          (n - 3) * (n - 4) / 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n =
        0 ∧
      KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u0 q n +
                4 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n +
              2 * (n - 4) * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n +
            2 * (n - 4) * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n +
          (n - 4) * (n - 5) / 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n =
        0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (J : Finset E) (r T : ℕ) (M n β b C A : ℝ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hM : 0 < M) (hT : ↑T < M)
  (hMb : M ≤ C * n ^ 2) (hsize : ∀ (w : B), (I w).card = r) (hJ : ↑J.card = ↑r * M)
  (hsupport : ∀ (w : B), ¬I w ⊆ J → μ w = 0)
  (hunit : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e = if e ∈ J then 1 else 0) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) (hb : 0 ≤ b)
  (hβrate : β ≤ b / n) (hcodeg : ∀ (e f : E), e ≠ f → KqR75.CodegreeLoss.codegree I μ e f ≤ β) (hA : 0 < A)
  (hquota : 4 * C * KqR75.PhysicalDrift.noiseConstant r b ≤ A)
  (hguards :
    ∀ (h : List (Option B)),
      h.length < T →
        KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h →
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
                (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M) h <

            KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M n β h) :

  have K :=
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J fun h =>
      KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M n β h ∧ h.length < T ∧ Y h < A * n;
  (KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T fun h => Option.none ∈ h ∨ KqR75.EnergyExit.crossed Y (A * n) h) ≤
    1 / 4

[GreedyHorizon] KqR75.GreedyHorizon.no_stop_without_exit
KqR75.GreedyHorizon.no_stop_without_exit.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b)
  (J : Finset E) (r T : ℕ) (M n β u : ℝ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hM : 0 < M) (hT : ↑T < M)
  (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (hJ : ↑J.card = ↑r * M)
  (hguards :
    ∀ (h : List (Option B)),
      h.length < T →
        KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h →
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
                (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M) h <
              u →
            KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M n β h) :

      KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M n β h ∧ h.length < T ∧ Y h < u;
  ∀ (h : List (Option B)),
    h.length ≤ T → 0 < LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h → ¬KqR75.EnergyExit.crossed Y u h → Option.none ∉ h

[GreedyScales] KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon_bounds

  have T := KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a;

[GreedyScales] KqR75.GreedyScales.type_drift_sums
KqR75.GreedyScales.type_drift_sums (n T r : ℕ) (M C a z : ℝ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hr : 2 ≤ r) (hM : 0 < M)
  (hC : 0 ≤ C) (hbound : M ≤ C * ↑n ^ 2) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (_hz : 0 ≤ z) (hscale : a * (4 * ↑r - 1) = 1 - 2 * z)
  (hT : T ≤ KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a) :
  have S := fun s => ∑ i ∈ Finset.range T, ((M - ↑i) / M) ^ (-s);
  ↑n ^ (-3) * S (4 * ↑r) ≤ C / (4 * ↑r - 1) * ↑n ^ (-2 * z) ∧
    ↑n ^ (-5 / 2) * S (2 * ↑r + 1 / 2) ≤ C / (2 * ↑r - 1 / 2) * ↑n ^ (-z) ∧
      ↑n ^ (-3) * S ↑r ≤ C / (↑r - 1) * ↑n ^ (-z) ∧
        ↑n ^ (-4) * S (2 * ↑r) ≤ C / (2 * ↑r - 1) * ↑n ^ (-2 + a * (2 * ↑r - 1)) ∧
          -2 + a * (2 * ↑r - 1) ≤ -z
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[GreedyScales] KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent_bounds
KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent_bounds (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) :
  have z := KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q;
  have a := (2 * ↑q - 1) * z;
  0 < z ∧ z ≤ 1 ∧ 0 < a ∧ a * (4 * ↑(q.choose 2) - 1) = 1 - 2 * z
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[GreedyScales] KqR75.GreedyScales.sum_inverse_remaining_power

  have p := fun i => (M - ↑i) / M;

[GreedyScales] KqR75.GreedyScales.eventually_guard_scales

                64 * ↑r ^ 2 * β ≤ ((M - ↑i) / M) ^ (r - 1) ∧

[GreedySchedule] KqR75.GreedySchedule.eventually_regular
KqR75.GreedySchedule.eventually_regular.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (r : ℕ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) {a z c b A : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hz : 0 < z)
  (hscale : a * (4 * ↑r - 1) = 1 - 2 * z) (hc : 0 < c) :
  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    ∀ (M β : ℝ),
      c * ↑n ^ 2 ≤ M →
        β ≤ b / ↑n →
          ∀ (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)),
            KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h →
              KqR75.WeightedGreedy.selectedCount h ≤ KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a →
                KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
                      (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M) h ≤
                    A * ↑n →
                  KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M (↑n) β h
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (ih : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.selectedCount h ≤ h.length) :
  ¬KqR75.WeightedGreedy.selectedCount h + 1 ≤ h.length + 1 → False
Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

[HorizonConcentration] KqR75.HorizonConcentration.count_rate
KqR75.HorizonConcentration.count_rate (n C z : ℝ) (T : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hC : 0 < C) (hz : z ≤ 1 / 2)
  (hT : 0 < T) (hbound : ↑T ≤ C * n ^ 2) :
  2 * Real.exp (-((n ^ (2 - z)) ^ 2 / (2 * ↑T))) ≤ 2 * Real.exp (-(1 / (2 * C)) * n)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[HorizonConcentration] KqR75.HorizonConcentration.prefix_tail
KqR75.HorizonConcentration.prefix_tail.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (w : B), 0 ≤ μ w) (J : Finset E)
  (S : Finset B) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (r T : ℕ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (w : B), (I w).card = r)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1) (g : ℕ → ℝ) (hg : ∀ (i : ℕ), 0 < g i)
  (hregular : ∀ (h : List (Option B)), guard h → 2 * ↑r ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h)
  (hdenom : ∀ (h : List (Option B)), guard h → g h.length ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h)
  (u L V : ℝ) (hu : 0 < u) (hL : 0 ≤ L) (hV : 0 < V) (hv : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range T, (4 * ↑r / g i) ^ 2 ≤ V) :
  have K := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard;
  have drift := fun h =>
    LatinonEntropy.pathSum
      (fun q x => ∑ w, K.transition q w * KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment I μ J S guard q w) h;
  (∀ (h : List (Option B)), h.length ≤ T → |drift h| ≤ L) →
    ∀ t ≤ T,
      (KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K t fun h =>
          u + L < |KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S h - KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S []|) ≤
        2 * Real.exp (-(u ^ 2 / (2 * V)))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[HorizonConcentration] KqR75.HorizonConcentration.proportion_rate
KqR75.HorizonConcentration.proportion_rate (n : ℝ) (r : ℕ) (a z K : ℝ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (ha : 0 ≤ a)
  (hK : 0 < K) (hs : a * (4 * ↑r - 1) = 1 - 2 * z) :
  2 * Real.exp (-((n ^ (-z)) ^ 2 / (2 * (K * n ^ (-2 + a * (2 * ↑r - 1)))))) ≤
    2 * Real.exp (-(1 / (2 * K)) * n)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[HorizonCountTheorem] KqR75.HorizonCountTheorem.eventually_count
KqR75.HorizonCountTheorem.eventually_count.{u_3, u_4} (r : ℕ) (hr : 2 ≤ r) (k : ℕ) (c C A b a z : ℝ)
  (hc : 0 < c) (hC : 0 < C) (hA : 0 < A) (hb : 0 ≤ b) (ha : 0 < a) (hz : 0 < z) (hz₁ : z ≤ 1 / 2)
  (hs : a * (4 * ↑r - 1) = 1 - 2 * z) (hquota : 4 * C * KqR75.PhysicalDrift.noiseConstant r b ≤ A) :
  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    ∀ (E : Type u_3) (B : Type u_4) [inst : Fintype E] [inst_1 : DecidableEq E] [inst_2 : Fintype B]
      [inst_3 : DecidableEq B] (M β : ℝ) (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (w : B), 0 ≤ μ w)
      (J : Finset E) (F : Fin k → Finset B),
      c * ↑n ^ 2 ≤ M →
        M ≤ C * ↑n ^ 2 →
          0 ≤ β →
            β ≤ b / ↑n →
              (∀ (w : B), (I w).card = r) →
                ↑J.card = ↑r * M →
                  (∀ (w : B), ¬I w ⊆ J → μ w = 0) →
                    (∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e = if e ∈ J then 1 else 0) →
                      (∀ (e f : E), e ≠ f → KqR75.CodegreeLoss.codegree I μ e f ≤ β) →
                        have T := KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a;
                        have Y := KqR75.HorizonCountTheorem.energy I μ J r M;
                        have K :=
                          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J
                            (KqR75.HorizonCountTheorem.guard I μ J r T M (↑n) β (A * ↑n));
                        1 / 2 ≤
                          KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T fun h =>
                            Option.none ∉ h ∧
                              KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h ∧
                                ¬KqR75.EnergyExit.crossed Y (A * ↑n) h ∧
                                  ∀ (d : Fin k),
                                    |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count (F d) h) -
                                          ↑T * KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J (F d) []| ≤
                                      (1 + C * (1 + KqR75.CumulativeTypeDrift.driftConstant r c C A b)) *
                                        ↑n ^ (2 - z)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

  (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hz : 0 ≤ z) (hc : 0 < c) (hnc : 4 * ↑r / c ≤ ↑n) (hs : a * (4 * ↑r - 1) = 1 - 2 * z)

  {n q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ)

    KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs PC

[HostCorrection] KqR75.HostCorrection.repair_positive

  (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t u δ : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (hδ : 0 < δ)

[IncidenceAlgebra] KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.inverse_from_incidence
KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.inverse_from_incidence.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype V] [DecidableEq V] (T : Matrix E V ℝ) (q n : ℝ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hn : q + 2 ≤ n)
  (hsize : ↑(Fintype.card V) = n) (hrow : ∀ (e : E), ∑ v, T e v = 2) (hcol : ∀ (v : V), ∑ e, T e v = n - 1)
  (hgram : T.transpose * T = (n - 2) • 1 + KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.ones) :
  have K := T * T.transpose;
  have J := KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.ones;
  ((1 - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) • 1 +
          (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) • K +
        KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n • J) *
      ((KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u0 q n - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) • 1 +
          (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) • K +
        KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n • J) =
    1
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[IncidenceAlgebra] KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.relations
KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.relations.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype V] [DecidableEq V] (T : Matrix E V ℝ) (n : ℝ) (hsize : ↑(Fintype.card V) = n)
  (hrow : ∀ (e : E), ∑ v, T e v = 2) (hcol : ∀ (v : V), ∑ e, T e v = n - 1)
  (hgram : T.transpose * T = (n - 2) • 1 + KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.ones) :
  have K := T * T.transpose;
  have J := KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.ones;
  K * K = (n - 2) • K + 4 • J ∧
    K * J = (2 * (n - 1)) • J ∧ J * K = (2 * (n - 1)) • J ∧ J * J = (n * (n - 1) / 2) • J
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[IncidenceAlgebra] KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.gram_row_sum
KqR75.IncidenceAlgebra.gram_row_sum.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype V] [DecidableEq V] (T : Matrix E V ℝ) (n : ℝ) (hrow : ∀ (e : E), ∑ v, T e v = 2)
  (hcol : ∀ (v : V), ∑ e, T e v = n - 1) (e : E) : ∑ f, (T * T.transpose) e f = 2 * (n - 1)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[JohnsonAlgebra] KqR75.JohnsonAlgebra.basis_product
KqR75.JohnsonAlgebra.basis_product.{u_1} {E : Type u_1} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E] (K J : Matrix E E ℝ)
  (l d t N : ℝ) (hKK : K * K = l • K + d • J) (hKJ : K * J = t • J) (hJK : J * K = t • J)
  (hJJ : J * J = N • J) (a b c A B C : ℝ) :
  (a • 1 + b • K + c • J) * (A • 1 + B • K + C • J) =
    (a * A) • 1 + (a * B + b * A + l * b * B) • K +
      (a * C + c * A + d * b * B + t * (b * C + c * B) + N * c * C) • J
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[JohnsonAlgebra] KqR75.JohnsonAlgebra.inverse_coefficients
KqR75.JohnsonAlgebra.inverse_coefficients (q n : ℝ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hn : q + 2 ≤ n) :
  (1 - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) *
        (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u0 q n - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) =
      1 ∧
    (1 - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) *
              (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) +
            (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) *
              (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u0 q n - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n +
                KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) +
          (n - 2) * (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) *
            (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) =
        0 ∧
      (1 - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n +
                KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n *
                  (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u0 q n - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n +
                    KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) +
              4 * (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) *
                (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) +
            2 * (n - 1) *
              ((KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n +
                KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n * (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n)) +
          n * (n - 1) / 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n =
        0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[JohnsonAlgebra] KqR75.JohnsonAlgebra.inverse_of_relations
KqR75.JohnsonAlgebra.inverse_of_relations.{u_1} {E : Type u_1} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  (K J : Matrix E E ℝ) (q n : ℝ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hn : q + 2 ≤ n) (hKK : K * K = (n - 2) • K + 4 • J)
  (hKJ : K * J = (2 * (n - 1)) • J) (hJK : J * K = (2 * (n - 1)) • J)
  (hJJ : J * J = (n * (n - 1) / 2) • J) :
  ((1 - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) • 1 +
          (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n) • K +
        KqR75.GeneralJohnson.s2 q n • J) *
      ((KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u0 q n - 2 * KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n + KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) • 1 +
          (KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u1 q n - KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n) • K +
        KqR75.GeneralJohnson.u2 q n • J) =
    1
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[JohnsonScalars] KqR75.Johnson.orbit_identities
KqR75.Johnson.orbit_identities (n : ℝ) (hn : 6 ≤ n) :
  KqR75.Johnson.u0 n + 2 * (n - 2) * KqR75.Johnson.s1 n * KqR75.Johnson.u1 n +

        0 ∧
      KqR75.Johnson.u2 n + KqR75.Johnson.s2 n * KqR75.Johnson.u0 n +
                4 * KqR75.Johnson.s1 n * KqR75.Johnson.u1 n +

        0

[JointCorrection] KqR75.JointCorrection.delta.eq_1
KqR75.JointCorrection.delta.eq_1.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} {D : Type u_3} [Fintype E]
  [Fintype B] [Fintype D] [DecidableEq D] (R : Matrix B E ℝ) (H : Matrix D B ℝ) (L : Matrix B D ℝ)
  (h : E → ℝ) (t : D → ℝ) :
  KqR75.JointCorrection.delta R H L h t =
    R.mulVec h + L.mulVec ((H * L)⁻¹.mulVec (t - H.mulVec (R.mulVec h)))

  [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] [Fintype D] [DecidableEq D] (A : Matrix E B ℝ)
  (R : Matrix B E ℝ) (H : Matrix D B ℝ) (L₀ : Matrix B D ℝ) (hR : A * R = 1) :
  have L := L₀ - R * (A * L₀);

[JointCorrection] KqR75.JointCorrection.repair_positive
KqR75.JointCorrection.repair_positive.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} {D : Type u_3}
  [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] [Fintype D] [DecidableEq D] (A : Matrix E B ℝ)

  (hnear : ∀ (i : D), ∑ j, |(1 - H * L) i j| ≤ 1 / 2) (CR CL u w ε : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (hw : 0 ≤ w)
  (hε : 0 < ε) (hrowsR : ∀ (b : B), ∑ e, |R b e| ≤ CR) (hrowsL : ∀ (b : B), ∑ i, |L b i| ≤ CL) (μ : B → ℝ)
  (ν : E → ℝ) (τ : D → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), ε ≤ μ b) (hdef : ∀ (e : E), |(ν - A.mulVec μ) e| ≤ u)
  (htype : ∀ (i : D), |(τ - H.mulVec μ - H.mulVec (R.mulVec (ν - A.mulVec μ))) i| ≤ w)
  (hsmall : CR * u + CL * (8 * w) ≤ ε / 2) :
  ∃ μ',
    A.mulVec μ' = ν ∧
      H.mulVec μ' = τ ∧ (∀ (b : B), 0 < μ' b) ∧ ∀ (b : B), |μ' b - μ b| ≤ CR * u + CL * (8 * w)
