[LowerAssembly] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_lower_of_omni
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_lower_of_omni.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ρ κ : ℝ) (hρ : 0 < ρ)
  (hκ : 0 < κ) (ref : C) (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s)
  (homni : KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.PhysicalOmniPlacement q (KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q)) :
  ∃ err,
    0 ≤ err ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ),
          (∀ (c : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c)) →
            (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
                  ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliques colour Finset.univ
                      (KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e))) →
              (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                  κ * (↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑(q.choose 2)) ≤ ↑(m t)) →
                q.choose 2 ∣ n.choose 2 →
                  q - 1 ∣ n - 1 →
                    have r := q.choose 2;
                    have z := KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q;
                    have B := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r;
                    0 < (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ∧
                      -∑ t,
                                ↑(m t) *
                                  Real.log
                                    (↑(m t) /
                                      ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour)
                                            t).card) -
                            (↑r - 1) * B -
                          err * (↑n ^ (2 - z) * Real.log ↑n) ≤
                        Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[LowerFromHost] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.lower_from_host
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.lower_from_host.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hnq : q ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i)
  (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (w : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ)
  (hw : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), 0 ≤ w b)
  (guard : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) → Prop)
  (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ) (ℓ W E : ℝ) (hℓ : 0 < ℓ) (hW : 0 < W) (hE : 0 ≤ E)
  (hseedlo :
    ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
      ℓ * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q ≤
        KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) (fun t => ↑(m t)) b)
  (hseedhi :
    ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
      KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) (fun t => ↑(m t)) b ≤
        W * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q)
  (c A a z D : ℝ) (hc : 0 < c) (hA : 0 ≤ A)
  (hpert :
    ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
      |w b - KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) (fun t => ↑(m t)) ↑b| ≤
        KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q * (E * ↑n ^ (-z)))
  (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hz : z ≤ 2) (haz : z ≤ a) (hD : 0 ≤ D) (hscale : a * (4 * ↑(q.choose 2) - 1) = 1 - 2 * z)
  (good : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) → Prop) :
  have r := q.choose 2;
  have M := ↑J.card / ↑r;
  have B := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r;
  have T := KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a;
  let μ := KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J w;
  have K :=
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) μ
      (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.extend_nonneg J w hw) J guard;
  have V := 2 * (E / ℓ) + 2 * √(A / (↑r * c));
  have L₀ := |Real.log ℓ| + |Real.log W|;
  have err :=
    (3 + V) / Real.log 2 + 3 +
      ↑(Fintype.card (KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q)) * D * (L₀ / Real.log 2 + ↑(q - 2));
  c * ↑n ^ 2 ≤ M →
    1 ≤ T →
      E * ↑n ^ (-z) / ℓ ≤ 1 / 2 →
        √(A / (↑r * c)) * ↑n ^ (-z) ≤ 1 / 2 →
          1 / 2 ≤ KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T good →
            (∀ h ∈ LatinonEntropy.histories (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) T,
                good h →
                  0 < LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h →
                    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) J h ∧
                      Option.none ∉ h ∧
                        ¬KqR75.EnergyExit.crossed
                              (KqR75.HorizonCountTheorem.energy (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) μ J r M)
                              (A * ↑n) h ∧
                          (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                              |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour t) h) -
                                    ↑(m t)| ≤
                                D * ↑n ^ (2 - z)) ∧
                            ∃ P,
                              KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P Finset.univ ∧
                                (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                    KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour P t = m t) ∧
                                  {b ∈ P | KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ⊆ J} =
                                    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h) →
              0 < (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ∧
                -∑ t,
                          ↑(m t) *
                            Real.log
                              (↑(m t) /
                                ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour)
                                      t).card) -
                      (↑r - 1) * B -
                    err * (↑n ^ (2 - z) * Real.log ↑n) ≤
                  Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_lower_geometry._proof_1✝ (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (B : ℤ) : ¬2 ≤ q → False

[LowerGeometry] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_lower_geometry._proof_2

Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

[LowerGeometry] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_lower_geometry
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_lower_geometry.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ρ : ℝ) (hρ : 0 < ρ) (ref : C)
  (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s) (tag : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q → C)
  (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q) (B : ℤ) (hB : 0 ≤ B) (coeff E₂ : ℝ) (hcoeff : 0 ≤ coeff)
  (hE₂ : 0 ≤ E₂)
  (homni : KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.PhysicalOmniPlacement q (KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q)) :
  have r := q.choose 2;
  have z := KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q;
  have a := (2 * ↑q - 1) * z;
  ∃ U G T,
    0 < U ∧
      0 < G ∧
        0 < T ∧
          ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
            ∀ (colour : Fin n → C),
              (∀ (c : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c)) →
                have ε := ⌈(coeff * U + E₂ + G + U) * ↑n ^ (2 - z)⌉₊;
                have M := B.toNat * ε;
                ∃ A X F f,
                  Disjoint A X ∧
                    Disjoint A F ∧
                      Disjoint X F ∧
                        (∀ (t : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (i : Fin q) (x : K),
                            colour ((f t) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag t.1 i else ref) ∧
                          (∀ (i j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M),
                              i ≠ j →
                                (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) ∧
                            KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs
                                (KqR75.PhysicalBank.basalBlocks
                                  (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_lower_geometry._proof_2 q hq B) j s
                                  hs f)
                                F ∧
                              (∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ U * ↑n ^ (1 - z)) ∧
                                (∀ (v : Fin n),
                                    ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ G * ↑n ^ (1 - z)) ∧
                                  (∀ (v : Fin n),
                                      ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ T * ↑n ^ (1 - z)) ∧
                                    (∀ S ⊆ X,
                                        KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.Divisible q S →
                                          ∃ P, KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P (A ∪ S)) ∧
                                      ∀ (L : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                                        ↑L.card ≤ 2 * ↑r * ↑n ^ (2 - a) →
                                          Disjoint L X →
                                            ∃ PC,
                                              ∃ Y ⊆ X,
                                                KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs PC (L ∪ Y) ∧
                                                  ∀ b ∈ PC, (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ∩ X).Nonempty
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[LowerHostEvent] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_lower_with_recovering_host
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_lower_with_recovering_host.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ℓ W : ℝ) (hℓ : 0 < ℓ) (hW : 0 < W) :
  ∃ D,
    0 ≤ D ∧
      ∀ (E R U : ℝ),
        0 ≤ E →
          0 ≤ R →
            0 ≤ U →
              ∃ err,
                0 ≤ err ∧
                  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
                    ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ)
                      (P₀ : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))
                      (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))
                      (w : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ),
                      (∀ (i : C), q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i) →
                        ↑n ^ 2 / 4 ≤ ↑J.card →
                          (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
                              0 ≤ w b ∧ w b ≤ W * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q) →
                            ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w = fun x => 1) →
                              (∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
                                  ℓ * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q ≤
                                      KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour)
                                        (fun t => ↑(m t)) b ∧
                                    KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour)
                                        (fun t => ↑(m t)) b ≤
                                      W * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q) →
                                have r := q.choose 2;
                                have M := ↑J.card / ↑r;
                                have B := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r;
                                have z := KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q;
                                have a := (2 * ↑q - 1) * z;
                                have T := KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a;
                                have μ := KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J w;
                                (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
                                    |w b -
                                          KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight
                                            (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) (fun t => ↑(m t))
                                            ↑b| ≤
                                      KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q * (E * ↑n ^ (-z))) →
                                  (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                      |↑(m t) - ↑(KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour P₀ t) -
                                            KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w t| ≤
                                        R * ↑n ^ (2 - z)) →
                                    ↑P₀.card ≤ U * ↑n ^ (2 - z) →
                                      (∀ (h : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))),
                                          h.length = T →
                                            Option.none ∉ h →
                                              KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) J
                                                  h →
                                                (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                                    |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count
                                                              (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour t)
                                                              h) -
                                                          ↑T *
                                                            KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion
                                                              (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) μ J
                                                              (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour t)
                                                              []| ≤
                                                      D * ↑n ^ (2 - z)) →
                                                  ∃ P,
                                                    KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P Finset.univ ∧
                                                      (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                                          KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour P t =
                                                            m t) ∧
                                                        {b ∈ P | KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ⊆ J} =
                                                          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h) →
                                        0 < (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ∧
                                          -∑ t,
                                                    ↑(m t) *
                                                      Real.log
                                                        (↑(m t) /
                                                          ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre
                                                                (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour)
                                                                t).card) -
                                                (↑r - 1) * B -
                                              err * (↑n ^ (2 - z) * Real.log ↑n) ≤
                                            Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[LowerHostScales] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.critical_completion_scales
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.critical_completion_scales (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) :
  have r := q.choose 2;
  have z := KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q;
  have a := (2 * ↑q - 1) * z;
  0 < z ∧ z < 1 / 2 ∧ z ≤ a ∧ a ≤ 1 ∧ a * (4 * ↑r - 1) = 1 - 2 * z ∧ (2 * ↑q + 1) * z < 1
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[LowerHostScales] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.horizon_leave_for_cover
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.horizon_leave_for_cover {n q : ℕ} (hn : 1 ≤ n) (a : ℝ) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (ha' : a ≤ 2)
  (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (M : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M) (hMup : M ≤ ↑n ^ 2)
  (hcard : ↑J.card = ↑(q.choose 2) * M) (h : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)))
  (hlen : h.length = KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a)
  (hv : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) J h) (hstop : Option.none ∉ h) :
  ↑(KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) J h).card ≤
    2 * ↑(q.choose 2) * ↑n ^ (2 - a)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[LowerSeedInputs] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_interior_seed
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_interior_seed.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] (q : ℕ)
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ρ κ : ℝ) (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hκ : 0 < κ) :
  have s := ρ / 4;
  have W := ↑q.factorial ^ Fintype.card C / s ^ q;
  have ℓ := κ / (4 * ↑(q.choose 2)) / (2 ^ (q - 2) * ↑(q - 2).factorial);
  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ),
      (∀ (i : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i)) →
        (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
            κ * (↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑(q.choose 2)) ≤ m t) →
          (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
              KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour m e =
                ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliques colour Finset.univ
                    (KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e))) →
            ∃ d,
              (∀ (i : C), d + q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i) ∧
                s * ↑n ≤ ↑d ∧
                  (∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
                      ℓ * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q ≤
                        KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) m b) ∧
                    (∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
                        |KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) m b| ≤
                          W * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q) ∧
                      ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                        KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q)
                            (KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) m) e =
                          1
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[LowerSeedInputs] _private.LowerSeedInputs.0.KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_interior_seed._proof_1_4

Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

  {n q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (colour : Fin n → C)

[MeanEnergy] KqR75.MeanEnergy.fixedChange.eq_1
KqR75.MeanEnergy.fixedChange.eq_1.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] (I : B → Finset E) (w : B → ℝ) (x : E → ℝ) (c : B) :
  KqR75.MeanEnergy.fixedChange I w x c =
    ∑ e,
      if e ∈ I c then -x e ^ 2
      else -2 * x e * KqR75.OverlapDrift.loss I w c e + KqR75.OverlapDrift.loss I w c e ^ 2
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[MeanEnergy] KqR75.MeanEnergy.load_square_error
KqR75.MeanEnergy.load_square_error.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)) (k : ℝ)
  (e : E) :
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e ^ 2 * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e =
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e ^ 2 +
      k * (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[MeanEnergy] _private.MeanEnergy.0.KqR75.MeanEnergy.physical_fixed_drift._simp_1_1
KqR75.MeanEnergy.physical_fixed_drift._simp_1_1✝.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E)
  (h : List (Option B)) (c : B) (e : E) :
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.loss I μ J h c e =
    KqR75.OverlapDrift.loss I (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h) c e
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[MeanEnergy] KqR75.MeanEnergy.fixedChange.congr_simp
KqR75.MeanEnergy.fixedChange.congr_simp.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  {inst✝ : DecidableEq E} [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] (I I✝ : B → Finset E) (e_I : I = I✝) (w w✝ : B → ℝ)
  (e_w : w = w✝) (x x✝ : E → ℝ) (e_x : x = x✝) (c c✝ : B) (e_c : c = c✝) :
  KqR75.MeanEnergy.fixedChange I w x c = KqR75.MeanEnergy.fixedChange I✝ w✝ x✝ c✝
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[MeanEnergy] KqR75.MeanEnergy.physical_fixed_drift
KqR75.MeanEnergy.physical_fixed_drift.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)) (r : ℕ)
  (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (k : ℝ) :
  have w := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h;
  have x := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k;
  ∑ c,
      w c *
        (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J (Option.some c :: h) k - KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J h k) =
    ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e * x e ^ 2 - 2 * ∑ b, w b * (∑ e ∈ I b, x e) ^ 2 -
          2 * (↑r - 1) * k * ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e * x e +
        2 * ∑ e, KqR75.OverlapDrift.correction I w e * x e +
      ∑ c, w c * KqR75.MeanEnergy.squareLoss I w c
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[MeanEnergy] KqR75.MeanEnergy.squareLoss.eq_1
KqR75.MeanEnergy.squareLoss.eq_1.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] (I : B → Finset E) (w : B → ℝ) (c : B) :
  KqR75.MeanEnergy.squareLoss I w c = ∑ e, if e ∈ I c then 0 else KqR75.OverlapDrift.loss I w c e ^ 2
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[MeanEnergy] KqR75.MeanEnergy.block_load_error
KqR75.MeanEnergy.block_load_error.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)) (r : ℕ)
  (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (k : ℝ) :
  ∑ b,
      (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h b * ∑ e ∈ I b, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e) *
        ∑ e ∈ I b, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e =
    ∑ b,
        KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h b *
          (∑ e ∈ I b, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e) ^ 2 +
      ↑r * k * ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[MeanEnergy] KqR75.MeanEnergy.mean_fixed_change
KqR75.MeanEnergy.mean_fixed_change.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (w : B → ℝ) (x : E → ℝ) :
  ∑ c, w c * KqR75.MeanEnergy.fixedChange I w x c =
    -∑ e, KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e * x e ^ 2 -
        2 *
          (∑ b, (w b * ∑ e ∈ I b, x e) * ∑ e ∈ I b, KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e -
              ∑ e, KqR75.OverlapDrift.correction I w e * x e -
            ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e ^ 2 * x e) +
      ∑ c, w c * KqR75.MeanEnergy.squareLoss I w c
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[MeanEnergy] KqR75.MeanEnergy.squareLoss.congr_simp

[MeanEnergy] KqR75.MeanEnergy.load_error_product
KqR75.MeanEnergy.load_error_product.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)) (k : ℝ)

[OmniBridge] KqR75.OmniBridge.exists_omni
KqR75.OmniBridge.exists_omni (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) :
  ∃ C,
    1 ≤ C ∧
      ∃ ε,
        0 < ε ∧
          ∀ (n : ℕ) (F X : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (D t : ℝ),
            1 ≤ n →
              1 + t ≤ ε * ↑n →
                X ⊆ Fᶜ →
                  D ≤ ↑n / ↑C →
                    (∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ t) →
                      (∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ D) →
                        ∃ A W finish,
                          A ⊆ Fᶜ ∧
                            Disjoint X A ∧
                              (∀ (v : Fin n),
                                  ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤
                                    ↑C * Max.max D (RefinedAbsorption.Original2024.budget n 2)) ∧
                                RefinedAbsorption.Original2024.IsOmniAbsorberOn q 2 Finset.univ
                                  (KqR75.CoverDown.vertexSets X) (KqR75.CoverDown.vertexSets A) W finish
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[OverlapDrift] KqR75.OverlapDrift.weighted_loss
KqR75.OverlapDrift.weighted_loss.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (w : B → ℝ) (f : E → ℝ) :
  ∑ c, w c * ∑ e, KqR75.OverlapDrift.loss I w c e * f e =
    ∑ b, (w b * ∑ e ∈ I b, f e) * ∑ e ∈ I b, KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e -
      ∑ e, KqR75.OverlapDrift.correction I w e * f e
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[OverlapDrift] KqR75.OverlapDrift.correction_bounds
KqR75.OverlapDrift.correction_bounds.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (w : B → ℝ) (hw : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ w b) (γ : ℝ)
  (hδ : ∀ (b : B), KqR75.OverlapDrift.overcount I w b ≤ γ) (e : E) :
  0 ≤ KqR75.OverlapDrift.correction I w e ∧
    KqR75.OverlapDrift.correction I w e ≤ γ * KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[OverlapDrift] KqR75.OverlapDrift.intersection_card
KqR75.OverlapDrift.intersection_card.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (b c : B) :
  ↑(I b ∩ I c).card = (if Disjoint (I b) (I c) then 0 else 1) + KqR75.OverlapDrift.excess I b c
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[OverlapDrift] KqR75.OverlapDrift.overcount_identity
KqR75.OverlapDrift.overcount_identity.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (w : B → ℝ) (b : B) :
  ∑ e ∈ I b, KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e =
    (∑ c, if Disjoint (I b) (I c) then 0 else w c) + KqR75.OverlapDrift.overcount I w b

[PackingJoin] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.assemble
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.assemble {n q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (A X F Y : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))
  (hAX : Disjoint A X) (hAF : Disjoint A F) (hXF : Disjoint X F) (hY : Y ⊆ X)
  (h : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)))
  (Pcover Pabs Pbank : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))
  (hv : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) (A ∪ X ∪ F)ᶜ h)
  (hcover :
    KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs Pcover
      (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) (A ∪ X ∪ F)ᶜ h ∪ Y))
  (hmark : ∀ b ∈ Pcover, (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ∩ X).Nonempty)
  (habs : KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs Pabs (A ∪ X \ Y))
  (hbank : KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs Pbank F) :
  have J := (A ∪ X ∪ F)ᶜ;
  have P := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h ∪ Pcover ∪ Pabs ∪ Pbank;
  KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P Finset.univ ∧
    {b ∈ P | KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ⊆ J} = KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.blocks hq f s hs σ = KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.blocks hq f✝ s✝ (e_s ▸ hs) σ✝

[PhysicalAffineCell] KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.resources_iff_cross_pair

  (f : Fin q × K ↪ Fin n) (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s) (σ : Fin q → Equiv.Perm K)

[PhysicalAffineCell] KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.switch_cell
KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.switch_cell.{u_1, u_2} {K : Type u_1} {C : Type u_2} [Field K] [Fintype K]
  [DecidableEq K] [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (f : Fin q × K ↪ Fin n) (s : Fin q → K)
  (hs : Function.Injective s) (r : Fin q) (tag : Fin q → C) (ref : C) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (hcolour : ∀ (i : Fin q) (x : K), colour (f (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag i else ref) :
  have plus := KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.blocks hq f s hs fun x => Equiv.refl K;
  have minus :=
    KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.blocks hq f s hs fun i => if i = r then Equiv.swap 0 1 else Equiv.refl K;
  (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      {b ∈ plus | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card =
        {b ∈ minus | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card) ∧
    ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
      ↑{b ∈ plus | KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour b = t}.card -
          ↑{b ∈ minus | KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour b = t}.card =
        KqR75.SlotPairMargins.cellDifference s ref tag r t
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PhysicalAffineCell] KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.switch_disjoint_cells
KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.switch_disjoint_cells.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {C : Type u_2} [Field K]
  [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ} {I : Type u_3} (cells : Finset I)
  (hq : 2 ≤ q) (f : I → Fin q × K ↪ Fin n) (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s) (r : I → Fin q)
  (tag : I → Fin q → C) (flip : I → Bool) (ref : C) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (hcolour : ∀ j ∈ cells, ∀ (i : Fin q) (x : K), colour ((f j) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag j i else ref)
  (hsep :
    ∀ i ∈ cells,
      ∀ j ∈ cells, i ≠ j → (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) :
  have after := fun j =>
    KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.blocks hq (f j) s hs fun i =>
      if flip j = Bool.true ∧ i = r j then Equiv.swap 0 1 else Equiv.refl K;
  have before := fun j =>
    KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.blocks hq (f j) s hs fun i =>
      if ¬flip j = Bool.true ∧ i = r j then Equiv.swap 0 1 else Equiv.refl K;
  (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      {b ∈ cells.biUnion after | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card =
        {b ∈ cells.biUnion before | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card) ∧
    ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
      ↑{b ∈ cells.biUnion after | KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour b = t}.card -
          ↑{b ∈ cells.biUnion before | KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour b = t}.card =
        ∑ j ∈ cells,
          (if flip j = Bool.true then -1 else 1) *
            KqR75.SlotPairMargins.cellDifference s ref (tag j) (r j) t
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PhysicalBank] KqR75.PhysicalBank.place_copies
KqR75.PhysicalBank.place_copies.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {n q M : ℕ} (ref : C)
  (tag : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q → C) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (forbidden : Fin n → Finset (Fin n)) (degree cap budget : ℕ) (hcap : 0 < cap)
  (hforbid : ∀ (v : Fin n), (forbidden v).card ≤ degree)
  (hsym : ∀ (a b : Fin n), a ∈ forbidden b ↔ b ∈ forbidden a)
  (htotal : Fintype.card (KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) * (2 * M) * (q * Fintype.card K) ≤ budget * cap)
  (hspace :
    ∀ (c : C),
      budget + q * Fintype.card K * (degree + q * Fintype.card K * cap + 1) <
        KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c) :
  ∃ f,
    (∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (i : Fin q) (x : K),
        colour ((f j) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag j.1 i else ref) ∧
      (∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (x y : Fin q × K), x ≠ y → (f j) x ∉ forbidden ((f j) y)) ∧
        (∀ (i j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M),
            i ≠ j → (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1) ∧
          ∀ (v : Fin n),
            (Finset.univ.biUnion fun j =>
                  if v ∈ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ then Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ else ∅).card ≤
              q * Fintype.card K * cap
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PhysicalBank] KqR75.PhysicalBank.switch_copies
KqR75.PhysicalBank.switch_copies.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {n q M : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (ref : C)
  (tag : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q → C) (r : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → Fin q)
  (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (f : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M → Fin q × K ↪ Fin n)
  (hcolour :
    ∀ (j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M) (i : Fin q) (x : K),
      colour ((f j) (i, x)) = if x = 0 then tag j.1 i else ref)
  (hsep :
    ∀ (i j : KqR75.PhysicalBank.Copy q ref M),
      i ≠ j → (Finset.map (f i) Finset.univ ∩ Finset.map (f j) Finset.univ).card ≤ 1)
  (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ) (a : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → ℤ)
  (ha : ∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), |a j| ≤ ↑M)
  (hw :
    ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
      w t = ∑ j, a j * KqR75.SlotPairMargins.cellDifference s ref (tag j) (r j) t) :
  ∃ selected,
    have after :=
      selected.biUnion fun j =>
        KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.blocks hq (f j) s hs fun i =>
          if j.2.1 = Bool.true ∧ i = r j.1 then Equiv.swap 0 1 else Equiv.refl K;
    have before :=
      selected.biUnion fun j =>
        KqR75.PhysicalAffineCell.blocks hq (f j) s hs fun i =>
          if ¬j.2.1 = Bool.true ∧ i = r j.1 then Equiv.swap 0 1 else Equiv.refl K;
    (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
        {b ∈ after | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card =
          {b ∈ before | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card) ∧
      ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
        ↑{b ∈ after | KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour b = t}.card -
            ↑{b ∈ before | KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour b = t}.card =
          w t
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PhysicalCover] KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_physical
KqR75.CoverDown.eventually_cover_physical (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (z M : ℝ) (hz : 0 < z) (hM : 0 ≤ M)
  (hgap : (2 * ↑q + 1) * z < 1) :
  ∃ K₀,
    1 ≤ K₀ ∧
      ∀ K ≥ K₀,
        ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
          ∃ p ω,
            ↑p = K * ↑n ^ (-z) ∧
              KqR75.ReserveRandom.RootGood p ω (q - 1) ∧
                have X := KqR75.PhysicalReserve.edges ω;
                ∀ (L : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                  ↑L.card ≤ M * ↑n ^ (2 - (2 * ↑q - 1) * z) →
                    Disjoint L X →
                      ∃ P,
                        ∃ Y ⊆ X,
                          Y.card ≤ q.choose 2 * L.card ∧
                            (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                                {C ∈ P | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q C}.card =
                                  if e ∈ L ∪ Y then 1 else 0) ∧
                              ∀ C ∈ P, (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q C ∩ X).Nonempty
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PhysicalDrift] KqR75.PhysicalDrift.energy_drift
KqR75.PhysicalDrift.energy_drift.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (h : List (Option B)) (hlive : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard h)
  (r : ℕ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1) (m k η C β : ℝ) (hm : 1 ≤ m) (hk : 0 < k)
  (hk1 : k ≤ 1) (hC : 0 ≤ C) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) (hcard : ↑(KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining I J h).card = ↑r * m)
  (hcodeg :
    ∀ (e f : E),
      e ≠ f → KqR75.CodegreeLoss.codegree I (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h) e f ≤ β)
  (hsmall : 64 * ↑r ^ 2 * β ≤ k) (hZ : m * k / 2 ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h)
  (hnear : (2 * ↑r - 1) * k * m ≤ (2 * ↑r - 1 / 2) * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h) (hη0 : 0 ≤ η)
  (hη : η ≤ (↑r - 1) * k / m) (hprofile : (↑r - 1) * k / m - η ≤ C * k / m ^ 2) :
  ∑ a,
      (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard).transition h a *
        (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J (a :: h) (k - η) - KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J h k) ≤
    2 * ↑r * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J h k / m + 32 * ↑r * (↑r ^ 2 * β) ^ 2 +
          ↑r * (2 * C + 4 * (↑r - 1)) ^ 2 / m ^ 2 +
        ↑r * (↑r - 1) ^ 2 / m +
      ↑r ^ 3 * β
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  {inst✝ : DecidableEq E} [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I I✝ : B → Finset E) (e_I : I = I✝)
  (μ μ✝ : B → ℝ) (e_μ : μ = μ✝) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J J✝ : Finset E) (e_J : J = J✝)
  (guard guard✝ : List (Option B) → Prop) (e_guard : guard = guard✝) :
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard =
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I✝ μ✝ (Eq.ndrec (motive := fun μ => ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) hμ e_μ) J✝ guard✝

  (hw : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), 0 ≤ w b)

[PhysicalHorizonCounts] KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.initial_proportion
KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.initial_proportion.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (hq : 2 ≤ q) (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (w : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ)
  (hw : (KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w = fun x => 1)
  (d : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q) :
  KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) (KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J w) J
      (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour d) [] =
    KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w d / (↑J.card / ↑(q.choose 2))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PhysicalHorizonCounts] KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.eventually_count
KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.eventually_count.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] (q : ℕ)
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (W : ℝ) (hW : 0 ≤ W) :
  ∃ D,
    ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
      ∀ (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (colour : Fin n → C)
        (w : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ)
        (hμ : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), 0 ≤ w b),
        ↑n ^ 2 / 4 ≤ ↑J.card →
          (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
              w b ≤ W * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q) →
            ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w = fun x => 1) →
              have r := q.choose 2;
              have M := ↑J.card / ↑r;
              have z := KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q;
              have a := (2 * ↑q - 1) * z;
              have T := KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a;
              let μ := KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J w;
              have I := KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q;
              have b := 2 * W * ↑q;
              have A := 1 + 4 * KqR75.PhysicalDrift.noiseConstant r b;
              have K :=
                KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.extend_nonneg J w hμ) J
                  (KqR75.HorizonCountTheorem.guard I μ J r T M (↑n) (b / ↑n) (A * ↑n));
              1 / 2 ≤
                KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T fun h =>
                  Option.none ∉ h ∧
                    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h ∧
                      ¬KqR75.EnergyExit.crossed (KqR75.HorizonCountTheorem.energy I μ J r M) (A * ↑n) h ∧
                        ∀ (d : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                          |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour d) h) -
                                ↑T *
                                  KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J
                                    (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour d) []| ≤
                            D * ↑n ^ (2 - z)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PhysicalHorizonCounts] _private.PhysicalHorizonCounts.0.KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.eventually_count._simp_1_2

[PhysicalHorizonCounts] KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion.eq_1

[PhysicalHorizonCounts] _private.PhysicalHorizonCounts.0.KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.eventually_count._proof_1_10

[PhysicalPrefixScore] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.physical_prefix_score
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.physical_prefix_score.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hnq : q ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i)
  (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (w' : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ)
  (hw' : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), 0 ≤ w' b)
  (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ) (ℓ W E : ℝ) (hℓ : 0 < ℓ) (hW : 0 < W) (hE : 0 ≤ E)
  (hwlo :
    ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
      ℓ * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q ≤
        KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) m b)
  (hwhi :
    ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
      KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) m b ≤
        W * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q)
  (hpert :
    ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
      |w' b - KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) m ↑b| ≤
        KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q * E)
  (hsmall : E / ℓ ≤ 1 / 2) (guard : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) → Prop) (c A a z D : ℝ)
  (hc : 0 < c) (hA : 0 ≤ A) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hD : 0 ≤ D) (hscale : a * (4 * ↑(q.choose 2) - 1) = 1 - 2 * z)
  (h : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))) :
  have M := ↑J.card / ↑(q.choose 2);
  let μ := KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J w';
  have K :=
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) μ
      (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.extend_nonneg J w' hw') J guard;
  have ε := √(A / (↑(q.choose 2) * c)) * ↑n ^ (-z);
  c * ↑n ^ 2 ≤ M →
    1 ≤ h.length →
      h.length ≤ KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a →
        Option.none ∉ h →
          0 < LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h →
            ¬KqR75.EnergyExit.crossed
                  (KqR75.HorizonCountTheorem.energy (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) μ J (q.choose 2) M)
                  (A * ↑n) h →
              (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                  |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour t) h) - m t| ≤ D) →
                ε ≤ 1 / 2 →
                  Real.log (LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h) + Real.log ↑h.length.factorial ≤
                    ∑ t,
                                  m t *
                                    Real.log
                                      (m t /
                                        ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre
                                              (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) t).card) +
                                (↑(q.choose 2) - 1) * M +
                              (M - ↑h.length) +
                            1 +
                          Real.log ↑h.length +
                        ↑h.length * (2 * (E / ℓ) + 2 * ε) +
                      ↑(Fintype.card (KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q)) *
                        (D * (|Real.log ℓ| + |Real.log W| + ↑(q - 2) * Real.log ↑n))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PhysicalPrefixTargets] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.prefix_counts_close_to_target
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.prefix_counts_close_to_target.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  {n q : ℕ} (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C) (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))

  (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ) (w : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ)
  (hw : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), 0 ≤ w b)
  (hload : (KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w = fun x => 1) (a z D K U : ℝ) (ha : 0 ≤ a)
  (hz : z ≤ 2) (haz : z ≤ a) (h : List (Option (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)))
  (hv : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) J h)
  (hh : h.length = KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon (↑J.card / ↑(q.choose 2)) n a)
  (hcount :
    ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
      |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour t) h) -
            ↑h.length *
              KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q)
                (KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J w) J (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour t) []| ≤
        D * ↑n ^ (2 - z))
  (hrepair :
    ∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
      |m t - ↑(KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour P t) -
            KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w t| ≤
        K * ↑n ^ (2 - z))
  (hP : ↑P.card ≤ U * ↑n ^ (2 - z)) (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q) :
  |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count (KqR75.PhysicalHorizonCounts.fibre colour t) h) - m t| ≤
    (D + 2 + K + U) * ↑n ^ (2 - z)

[PhysicalProfileLifts] KqR75.PhysicalProfileLifts.host_columns
KqR75.PhysicalProfileLifts.host_columns.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hn : 2 * q ^ 2 ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C) (ref : C) (d : ℕ) (s G : ℝ) (hs : 0 < s)
  (hG : 0 ≤ G) (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), d + q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i)
  (hdn : s * ↑n ≤ ↑d) (g : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ)
  (hg : ∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (v : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q), |g j v| ≤ G)
  (hcoord : ∀ (i j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), g j ↑i = if i = j then 1 else 0)
  (hmargin :
    ∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (i₁ i₂ : C),
      ∑ v, g j v * ↑(KqR75.SlotPairMargins.orderedCount v i₁ i₂) = 0)
  (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t : ℝ) (ht : 0 ≤ t)
  (hdeg : ∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ f, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑f) v ≤ t)
  (hsmall : 4 * (1 + ↑(q.choose 2)) * KqR75.SparseGram.outsideBound n q t ≤ 1 / 2) :
  have B := G * ↑q.factorial ^ Fintype.card C / s ^ q;
  have Q := B / ↑n ^ 2;
  have u := Q * (4 * ↑q ^ 2 * t / ↑n);
  have H := fun i b => if KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour ↑b = ↑i then 1 else 0;
  ∃ L,
    KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J * L = 0 ∧
      (∀ (i : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref),
          ∑ j, |(1 - H * L) i j| ≤
            ↑(Fintype.card (KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref)) * (Q * ↑Jᶜ.card + 8 * ↑J.card * u)) ∧
        ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)) (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref),
          |L b j - KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) (g j) ↑b| ≤
            8 * ↑(q.choose 2) * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q * u
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PrefixCount] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.completions_of_probability_cap
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.completions_of_probability_cap.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (J : Finset E)
  (hI : ∀ (b : B), (I b).Nonempty) (K : LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel (Option B)) (T : ℕ)
  (good : List (Option B) → Prop) (outputs : Finset (Finset B)) (complete : List (Option B) → Finset B)
  (M : ℝ) (hM : 0 ≤ M)
  (hgood :
    ∀ h ∈ LatinonEntropy.histories (Option B) T,
      good h →
        0 < LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h →
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h ∧
            Option.none ∉ h ∧
              complete h ∈ outputs ∧
                {b ∈ complete h | I b ⊆ J} = KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h ∧
                  LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h ≤ M) :
  KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T good ≤ ↑outputs.card * ↑T.factorial * M

[PrefixCount] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.log_historyWeight

  (K : LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel (Option B)) (h : List (Option B))
  (hp : 0 < LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h) :
  Real.log (LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h) =
    LatinonEntropy.pathSum (fun p b => Real.log (K.transition p b)) h
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PrefixCountFromLog] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.log_outputs_of_history_score
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.log_outputs_of_history_score.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (J : Finset E)
  (hI : ∀ (b : B), (I b).Nonempty) (K : LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel (Option B)) (T : ℕ)
  (good : List (Option B) → Prop) (outputs : Finset (Finset B)) (complete : List (Option B) → Finset B)
  (Q : ℝ) (hmass : 1 / 2 ≤ KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T good)
  (hgood :
    ∀ h ∈ LatinonEntropy.histories (Option B) T,
      good h →
        0 < LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h →
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h ∧
            Option.none ∉ h ∧
              complete h ∈ outputs ∧
                {b ∈ complete h | I b ⊆ J} = KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h ∧
                  Real.log (LatinonEntropy.historyWeight K h) + Real.log ↑T.factorial ≤ Q) :
  0 < outputs.card ∧ -Q - Real.log 2 ≤ Real.log ↑outputs.card
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PrefixLogSum] KqR75.GreedySchedule.normalizer_minus_factorial_lower
KqR75.GreedySchedule.normalizer_minus_factorial_lower (M : ℝ) (r T : ℕ) (hT₁ : 1 ≤ T) (hT : ↑T < M) :
  -↑r * M + ↑T - 1 - Real.log ↑T ≤
    ↑T * Real.log M + ↑r * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range T, Real.log ((M - ↑i) / M) - Real.log ↑T.factorial
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

KqR75.GreedySchedule.log_step✝ (x : ℝ) (hx : 0 < x) :

[PrefixMass] KqR75.GreedySchedule.relative_mass_error
KqR75.GreedySchedule.relative_mass_error.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (r : ℕ)
  (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (M : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M) (hJ : ↑J.card = ↑r * M)
  (h : List (Option B)) (hv : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h)
  (hi : ↑(KqR75.WeightedGreedy.selectedCount h) < M) (ε : ℝ) (hε : 0 ≤ ε) :
  have p := (M - ↑(KqR75.WeightedGreedy.selectedCount h)) / M;
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
        (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M) h ≤
      ε ^ 2 * (↑r * M * p ^ (4 * r - 1)) →
    |KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h - M * p ^ r| ≤ ε * (M * p ^ r)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PrefixMassScales] KqR75.GreedySchedule.relative_mass_at_horizon
KqR75.GreedySchedule.relative_mass_at_horizon.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (r n : ℕ)
  (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (M c A a z : ℝ) (hc : 0 < c) (hA : 0 ≤ A)
  (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hscale : a * (4 * ↑r - 1) = 1 - 2 * z) (hM : c * ↑n ^ 2 ≤ M) (hJ : ↑J.card = ↑r * M)
  (h : List (Option B)) (hv : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h)
  (hi : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.selectedCount h ≤ KqR75.GreedyScales.horizon M n a) :
  have p := (M - ↑(KqR75.WeightedGreedy.selectedCount h)) / M;
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
        (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M) h ≤
      A * ↑n →
    |KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h - M * p ^ r| ≤ √(A / (↑r * c)) * ↑n ^ (-z) * (M * p ^ r)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PrefixProbability] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.log_probability_with_ordering
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.log_probability_with_ordering.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2}
  {τ : Type u_3} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]
  (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop)
  (type : B → τ) (w target : τ → ℝ) (r : ℕ) (M D L εw εZ : ℝ) (hD : 0 ≤ D) (h : List (Option B))
  (hT₁ : 1 ≤ h.length) (hT : ↑h.length < M) (hn : Option.none ∉ h)
  (hp : 0 < LatinonEntropy.historyWeight (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard) h)
  (hw : ∀ (b : B), 0 < μ b → Real.log (μ b) ≤ w (type b) + εw)
  (hc : ∀ (t : τ), |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count {c | type c = t} h) - target t| ≤ D)
  (hlog : ∀ (t : τ), |w t| ≤ L)
  (hd :
    ∀ j < h.length,
      Real.log M + ↑r * Real.log ((M - ↑j) / M) - εZ ≤
        Real.log (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J (List.drop (h.length - j) h))) :
  Real.log (LatinonEntropy.historyWeight (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard) h) +
      Real.log ↑h.length.factorial ≤
    ∑ t, target t * w t + (↑r - 1) * M + (M - ↑h.length) + 1 + Real.log ↑h.length + ↑h.length * (εw + εZ) +
      ↑(Fintype.card τ) * (D * L)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

    ∀ h ∈ F,
      h.length = T ∧
        KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h ∧ Option.none ∉ h ∧ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.picked h = S) :
  F.card ≤ T.factorial
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PrefixScoreBudget] _private.PrefixScoreBudget.0.KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.prefix_score_error_budget._proof_1_1
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.prefix_score_error_budget._proof_1_1✝ {n T : ℕ} (hT : 1 ≤ T) : ¬0 < T → False
Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

[PrefixScoreBudget] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.prefix_score_error_budget
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.prefix_score_error_budget {n T : ℕ} (hn : 2 ≤ n) (hT : 1 ≤ T) (M z B V L k j : ℝ)
  (hz : z ≤ 2) (hB : 0 ≤ B) (hV : 0 ≤ V) (hL : 0 ≤ L) (hj : 0 ≤ j) (hTup : ↑T ≤ ↑n ^ 2)
  (hrem : M - ↑T ≤ 2 * ↑n ^ (2 - z)) :
  have X := ↑n ^ (2 - z);
  M - ↑T + 1 + Real.log ↑T + ↑T * (V * ↑n ^ (-z)) + j * (B * X * (L + k * Real.log ↑n)) + Real.log 2 ≤
    ((3 + V) / Real.log 2 + 3 + j * B * (L / Real.log 2 + k)) * (X * Real.log ↑n)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[PrefixTypeTests] KqR75.PrefixTypeTests.prefix_union
KqR75.PrefixTypeTests.prefix_union.{u_1, u_2} {Ω : Type u_1} {D : Type u_2} [Fintype Ω] [DecidableEq Ω]
  (K : LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel Ω) (T : ℕ) (S : Finset D) (P : D → ℕ → List Ω → Prop) (δ : ℝ)

    ↑S.card * (↑T + 1) * δ

[PrefixTypeTests] KqR75.PrefixTypeTests.counts_and_prefix_union

  [DecidableEq Ω] (K : LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel Ω) (T : ℕ) (S : Finset D) (P : D → ℕ → List Ω → Prop)

  (KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T fun h =>
      (∃ d ∈ S, ∃ t ≤ T, P d t (List.drop (T - t) h)) ∨ ∃ d ∈ S, Q d h) ≤
    ↑S.card * (↑T + 1) * δ + ↑S.card * η

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.load_extend
KqR75.ProfileBalance.load_extend {n q : ℕ} (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))
  (w : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ) (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) :
  KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) (KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J w) e =
    if h : e ∈ J then (KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w ⟨e, h⟩ else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram_eq_of_loads
KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram_eq_of_loads.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (hq : 2 ≤ q) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), 2 ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i) (ref : C)
  (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ)
  (v : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n) → ℝ)
  (hload :
    ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) w e = v e)
  (hmargin :
    ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour m e =
        ∑ f,
          v f *
            if KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑f = KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e then 1
            else 0)
  (hhigh : ∀ (t : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour w ↑t = m ↑t) :
  KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour w = m
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram.eq_1
KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram.eq_1.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (colour : Fin n → C) (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ)
  (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q) :
  KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour w t =
    ∑ b ∈ KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) t, w b

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram_extend
KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram_extend.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (colour : Fin n → C) (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))
  (w : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ) (v : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q) :
  KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w v =
    ∑ b, if KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour ↑b = v then w b else 0

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend.eq_1

  (w : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ) (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q) :
  KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J w b =
    if h : KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b ⊆ J then w ⟨b, KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend._proof_1 J b h⟩
    else 0

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.highIncidence.eq_1
KqR75.ProfileBalance.highIncidence.eq_1.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (colour : Fin n → C) (ref : C) (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.margin_histogram
KqR75.ProfileBalance.margin_histogram.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (colour : Fin n → C) (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ) (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) :
  KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour w) e =

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram.eq_1

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.repair_full_histogram
KqR75.ProfileBalance.repair_full_histogram.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (hq : 2 ≤ q) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), 2 ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i) (ref : C)
  (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))
  (R : Matrix ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) ↥J ℝ)
  (L : Matrix (↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)) (KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) ℝ)
  (hR : KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J * R = 1)
  (hL : KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J * L = 0)
  (hnear :
    ∀ (i : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref),
      ∑ j, |1 i j - (KqR75.ProfileBalance.highIncidence colour ref J * L) i j| ≤ 1 / 2)
  (CR CL u t ε : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (ht : 0 ≤ t) (hε : 0 < ε)
  (hrowsR : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), ∑ e, |R b e| ≤ CR)
  (hrowsL : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), ∑ i, |L b i| ≤ CL)
  (μ : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J) → ℝ) (ν : ↥J → ℝ)
  (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ) (hcompat : KqR75.ProfileBalance.compatible colour J m ν)
  (hμ : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), ε ≤ μ b)
  (hdef : ∀ (e : ↥J), |(ν - (KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec μ) e| ≤ u)
  (htype :
    ∀ (i : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref),
      |m ↑i - (KqR75.ProfileBalance.highIncidence colour ref J).mulVec μ i -
            (KqR75.ProfileBalance.highIncidence colour ref J).mulVec
              (R.mulVec (ν - (KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec μ)) i| ≤
        t)
  (hsmall : CR * u + CL * (8 * t) ≤ ε / 2) :
  ∃ μ',
    (KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec μ' = ν ∧
      KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J μ' = m ∧
        (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), 0 < μ' b) ∧
          ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), |μ' b - μ b| ≤ CR * u + CL * (8 * t)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ProfileBalance] KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend._proof_1

[ProfileLDPPrinciple] KqR75.ProfileLDP.countLDP_of_bounds
KqR75.ProfileLDP.countLDP_of_bounds.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι] {adm : ℕ → Prop} {total : ℕ → ℝ}
  {count : ℕ → Set (ι → ℝ) → ℝ} {speed : ℕ → ℝ} {Feas : Set (ι → ℝ)} {I : (ι → ℝ) → ℝ}
  (R : ℕ → Finset (ι → ℝ)) (w : ℕ → (ι → ℝ) → ℝ) (d : (ι → ℝ) → ℝ) (hI : Continuous I) (hd : Continuous d)
  (hd0 : ∀ (Q : ι → ℝ), 0 ≤ d Q) (hfeas : ∀ Q ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, d Q = 0 → Q ∈ Feas)
  (hsp : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 ≤ speed n) (htot : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 ≤ total n)
  (hcount_up : ∀ (n : ℕ) (A : Set (ι → ℝ)), count n A ≤ ∑ Q ∈ R n with Q ∈ A, w n Q)
  (hcount_low : ∀ (n : ℕ) (A : Set (ι → ℝ)), ∀ Q ∈ R n, Q ∈ A → w n Q ≤ count n A)
  (hR : ∀ (ε : ℝ), 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, adm n → ↑(R n).card ≤ Real.exp (speed n * ε))
  (hbox : ∀ (ε : ℝ), 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, adm n → ∀ Q ∈ R n, Q ∈ Set.Icc 0 1 ∧ d Q ≤ ε)
  (hw :
    ∀ (ε : ℝ),
      0 < ε →
        ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, adm n → ∀ Q ∈ R n, w n Q ≤ total n * Real.exp (-(speed n * (I Q - ε))))
  (hlow :
    ∀ Q ∈ Feas,
      (∀ (i : ι), 0 < Q i) →
        ∀ (ε : ℝ),
          0 < ε →
            ∀ V ∈ nhds Q,
              ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
                adm n → ∃ Q' ∈ R n, Q' ∈ V ∧ total n * Real.exp (-(speed n * (I Q + ε))) ≤ w n Q')
  (P : ι → ℝ) (hPpos : ∀ (i : ι), 0 < P i)
  (hconv : ∀ Q ∈ Feas, ∀ (s : ℝ), 0 ≤ s → s ≤ 1 → (fun i => (1 - s) * Q i + s * P i) ∈ Feas)
  (hFeas0 : ∀ Q ∈ Feas, ∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ Q i) : KqR75.ProfileLDP.CountLDP adm total count speed Feas I
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ProfileLDPPrinciple] KqR75.ProfileLDP.isCompact_rate_sublevel
KqR75.ProfileLDP.isCompact_rate_sublevel.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} {Feas : Set (ι → ℝ)} (hFeas : IsClosed Feas)
  (hbox : Feas ⊆ Set.Icc 0 1) {I : (ι → ℝ) → ℝ} (hI : Continuous I) (a : ℝ) :
  IsCompact {Q | Q ∈ Feas ∧ I Q ≤ a}

[PrunedWeights] KqR75.PrunedWeights.repair_signed_column

[PrunedWeights] KqR75.PrunedWeights.assemble_lifts
KqR75.PrunedWeights.assemble_lifts.{u_3} {n q : ℕ} {D : Type u_3} [Fintype D] [DecidableEq D] (hq : 3 ≤ q)
  (hn : 2 * q ^ 2 ≤ n) (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t C : ℝ) (ht : 0 ≤ t) (hC : 0 ≤ C)
  (hdeg : ∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ f, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑f) v ≤ t)
  (hsmall : 4 * (1 + ↑(q.choose 2)) * KqR75.SparseGram.outsideBound n q t ≤ 1 / 2)
  (w : D → ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.Block n q) → ℝ)
  (hw : ∀ (j : D) (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.Block n q)), |w j b| ≤ C * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q)
  (hzero :
    ∀ (j : D) (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) (w j) e = 0)
  (S : D → Finset ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.Block n q)) (hfull : ∀ (i j : D), ∑ b ∈ S i, w j b = 1 i j) :
  have u := C * (4 * ↑q ^ 2 * t / ↑n);
  have H := fun i b => if ↑b ∈ S i then 1 else 0;
  ∃ L,
    KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J * L = 0 ∧
      (∀ (i : D), ∑ j, |(1 - H * L) i j| ≤ ↑(Fintype.card D) * (C * ↑Jᶜ.card + 8 * ↑J.card * u)) ∧
        ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)) (j : D),
          |L b j - w j ↑b| ≤ 8 * ↑(q.choose 2) * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q * u
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[QuotaRounding] KqR75.SlotPairMargins.round_quotas
KqR75.SlotPairMargins.round_quotas.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s)
  (ref : C) :
  ∃ B,
    0 ≤ B ∧
      ∀ (m₀ : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ) (x : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ),
        (∀ (i j : C), ∑ v, (x v - ↑(m₀ v)) * ↑(KqR75.SlotPairMargins.orderedCount v i j) = 0) →
          ∃ m,
            (∀ (i j : C), ∑ v, (m v - m₀ v) * ↑(KqR75.SlotPairMargins.orderedCount v i j) = 0) ∧
              ∀ (v : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q), |↑(m v) - x v| ≤ B
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[QuotaRounding] _private.QuotaRounding.0.KqR75.SlotPairMargins.round_quotas._simp_1_1

[R75] KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_designFibre_bilateral
KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.eventually_designFibre_bilateral.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  [Nonempty C] (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ρ κ : ℝ) (hρ : 0 < ρ) (hκ : 0 < κ) :
  ∃ err,
    0 ≤ err ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ),
          (∀ (c : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c)) →
            KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.ExactPairMargins colour m →
              (∀ (t : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                  κ * (↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑(q.choose 2)) ≤ ↑(m t)) →
                q.choose 2 ∣ n.choose 2 →
                  q - 1 ∣ n - 1 →
                    have r := q.choose 2;
                    have z := KqR75.GreedyScales.criticalExponent q;
                    have s := ↑(r - 1);
                    have B := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r;
                    have N := fun t =>
                      ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) t).card;
                    have K := ∑ t, ↑(m t) / B * Real.log (↑(m t) / B / (N t / ↑(n.choose q)));
                    have main := B * (Real.log ↑((n - 2).choose (q - 2)) - s - K);
                    0 < (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ∧
                      main - err * (↑n ^ (2 - z) * Real.log ↑n) ≤
                          Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ∧
                        Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ≤
                          main + err * ↑n ^ (2 - 1 / s)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[RatioStep] KqR75.RatioStep.bounds
KqR75.RatioStep.bounds (Z A u v R : ℝ) (hR : 0 < R) (hZ : 2 * R ≤ Z) (hA0 : 0 ≤ A) (hA : A ≤ Z)
  (hu0 : 0 ≤ u) (hu : u ≤ R) (hv0 : 0 ≤ v) (hv : v ≤ u) :
  |(A - v) / (Z - u) - A / Z| ≤ 2 * R / Z ∧
    |(A - v) / (Z - u) - A / Z - (A / Z * u - v) / Z| ≤ 2 * R ^ 2 / Z ^ 2
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[RatioStep] KqR75.RatioStep.identity
KqR75.RatioStep.identity (Z A u v : ℝ) (hZ : Z ≠ 0) (hZu : Z - u ≠ 0) :
  (A - v) / (Z - u) - A / Z = (A / Z * u - v) / (Z - u)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[RatioStep] KqR75.RatioStep.physical_bounds
KqR75.RatioStep.physical_bounds.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (S : Finset B) (h : List (Option B)) (c : B) (r : ℕ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1)
  (hZ : 2 * ↑r ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h) :
  |KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S (Option.some c :: h) - KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S h| ≤
    2 * ↑r / KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h

[RepairedHostSeed] _private.RepairedHostSeed.0.KqR75.SeedScales.eventually_repaired_host._simp_1_1

[RepairedHostSeed] KqR75.SeedScales.eventually_repaired_host
KqR75.SeedScales.eventually_repaired_host.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] (q : ℕ)
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ref : C) (s G W a D U z : ℝ) (hs : 0 < s) (hG : 0 ≤ G) (hW : 0 ≤ W) (ha : 0 < a)
  (hD : 0 ≤ D) (hU : 0 ≤ U) (hz : 0 < z) :
  ∃ E,
    0 ≤ E ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (d : ℕ),
          (∀ (i : C), d + q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i) →
            s * ↑n ≤ ↑d →
              ∀ (g : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ),
                (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (v : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                    |g j v| ≤ G) →
                  (∀ (i j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), g j ↑i = if i = j then 1 else 0) →
                    (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (i₁ i₂ : C),
                        ∑ v, g j v * ↑(KqR75.SlotPairMargins.orderedCount v i₁ i₂) = 0) →
                      ∀ (P : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))
                        (J₀ X : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t tX : ℝ),
                        0 ≤ t →
                          0 ≤ tX →
                            t ≤ D * ↑n ^ (1 - z) →
                              tX ≤ U * ↑n ^ (1 - z) →
                                KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P J₀ᶜ →
                                  (∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ t) →
                                    (∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ tX) →
                                      ∀ (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ),
                                        (∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
                                            |w b| ≤ W * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q) →
                                          (∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
                                              a * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q ≤ w b) →
                                            (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                                                KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) w
                                                    e =
                                                  1) →
                                              let J := J₀ ∩ Xᶜ;
                                              have α := KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q;
                                              ∃ w',
                                                ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w' =
                                                    fun x => 1) ∧
                                                  (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
                                                      a * α / 4 ≤ w' b ∧ w' b ≤ (W + 1) * α) ∧
                                                    (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
                                                        |w' b - w ↑b| ≤ α * (E * ↑n ^ (-z))) ∧
                                                      ∀ (i : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                                        |KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour w i -
                                                                ↑(KqR75.BankCapacity.integerHistogram colour
                                                                    P i) -
                                                              KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w'
                                                                i| ≤
                                                          (W + 1) * (1 / 2 + 16 * ↑q ^ 2) * U * ↑n ^ (2 - z)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ReserveCuts] _private.ReserveCuts.0.KqR75.ReserveCuts.good_choices._proof_1_2
KqR75.ReserveCuts.good_choices._proof_1_2✝.{u_1} {V : Type u_1} [DecidableEq V] (R : V → V → Prop)
  [DecidableRel R] (p : ℝ) {t : ℕ} (U : Finset V) (W : Finset (Finset V))
  (hb :
    (W.biUnion fun S => {u ∈ U | ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.neighbours R S u).card < p / 2 * ↑S.card}).card ≤
      W.card * (t - 1))
  (he :
    (U \ W.biUnion fun S => {u ∈ U | ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.neighbours R S u).card < p / 2 * ↑S.card}).card +
        (W.biUnion fun S => {u ∈ U | ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.neighbours R S u).card < p / 2 * ↑S.card}).card =
      U.card) :
  ¬U.card ≤
        (U \
              W.biUnion fun S =>
                {u ∈ U | ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.neighbours R S u).card < p / 2 * ↑S.card}).card +
          W.card * (t - 1) →
    False
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  KqR75.ReserveCuts.embeddings R x✝ =

        Finset.map (KqR75.ReserveCuts.consEmbedding u)

[ReserveDeletion] KqR75.ReserveDeletion.eventually_repair_loads
KqR75.ReserveDeletion.eventually_repair_loads.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] (q : ℕ)
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (M a D Dₓ z : ℝ) (hM : 0 ≤ M) (ha : 0 < a) (hD : 0 ≤ D) (hDₓ : 0 ≤ Dₓ) (hz : 0 < z) :
  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (J₀ X : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t tX : ℝ),
      0 ≤ t →
        0 ≤ tX →
          t ≤ D * ↑n ^ (1 - z) →
            tX ≤ Dₓ * ↑n ^ (1 - z) →
              (∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ t) →
                (∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ tX) →
                  ∀ (w₀ : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J₀) → ℝ),
                    (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J₀)),
                        |w₀ b| ≤ M * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q) →
                      (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J₀)),
                          a * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q ≤ w₀ b) →
                        ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J₀).mulVec w₀ = fun x => 1) →
                          let J := J₀ ∩ Xᶜ;
                          have α := KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q;
                          ∃ w',
                            ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w' = fun x => 1) ∧
                              (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), a * α / 2 ≤ w' b) ∧
                                (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
                                    |w' b - KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J₀ w₀ ↑b| ≤
                                      α * (32 * ↑(q.choose 2) * M * ↑q ^ 2 * Dₓ * ↑n ^ (-z))) ∧
                                  ∀ (i : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                                    |KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J₀ w₀ i -
                                          KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w' i| ≤
                                      M * (1 / 2 + 16 * ↑q ^ 2) * Dₓ * ↑n ^ (2 - z)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ReserveDeletion] _private.ReserveDeletion.0.KqR75.ReserveDeletion.repair_loads._abel_1_1
KqR75.ReserveDeletion.repair_loads._abel_1_1✝ {n q : ℕ} (J₀ X : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))
  (w₀ : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J₀) → ℝ) :
  ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q (J₀ ∩ Xᶜ)).mulVec fun b =>
        KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J₀ w₀ ↑b) +
      ((fun x => 1) -
        (KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q (J₀ ∩ Xᶜ)).mulVec fun b =>
          KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J₀ w₀ ↑b) =
    fun x => 1

[ReserveDeletion] _private.ReserveDeletion.0.KqR75.ReserveDeletion.repair_loads._simp_1_2
KqR75.ReserveDeletion.repair_loads._simp_1_2✝.{u_1, u_5} {ι : Type u_1} {G : Type u_5} {s : Finset ι}
  [SubtractionCommMonoid G] (f g : ι → G) : ∑ x ∈ s, f x - ∑ x ∈ s, g x = ∑ x ∈ s, (f x - g x)
Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

[ReserveDeletion] KqR75.ReserveDeletion.repair_loads
KqR75.ReserveDeletion.repair_loads.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ} (hq : 3 ≤ q)
  (hn : 2 * q ^ 2 ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C) (J₀ X : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t tX M δ : ℝ)
  (htX : 0 ≤ tX) (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hdeg : ∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ t)
  (hdegX : ∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ e, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑e) v ≤ tX)
  (hsmall : 4 * (1 + ↑(q.choose 2)) * KqR75.SparseGram.outsideBound n q t ≤ 1 / 2)
  (w₀ : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J₀) → ℝ)
  (hw : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J₀)), |w₀ b| ≤ M * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q)
  (hwpos : ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J₀)), δ ≤ w₀ b)
  (hload : (KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J₀).mulVec w₀ = fun x => 1) :
  have J := J₀ ∩ Xᶜ;
  have u := M * (4 * ↑q ^ 2 * tX / ↑n);
  have c := 8 * ↑(q.choose 2) * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q * u;
  c ≤ δ / 2 →
    ∃ w',
      ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w' = fun x => 1) ∧
        (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), δ / 2 ≤ w' b) ∧
          (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
              |w' b - KqR75.ProfileBalance.extend J₀ w₀ ↑b| ≤ c) ∧
            ∀ (i : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
              |KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J₀ w₀ i -
                    KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w' i| ≤
                M * ↑X.card + 8 * ↑J.card * u
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ReserveRootLaws] KqR75.ReserveRandom.grouped_indicators
KqR75.ReserveRandom.grouped_indicators.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {V : Type u_1} [Fintype V] {I : Type u_2}
  {J : Type u_3} [Fintype I] [Fintype J] (p : ↑unitInterval) (f : (_ : I) × J ↪ Sym2 V) :
  have a :=
    ⟨↑p ^ Fintype.card J,
      ⟨pow_nonneg p.property.left (Fintype.card J), pow_le_one₀ p.property.left p.property.right⟩⟩;
  have Y := fun i ω => if ∀ (j : J), ω (f ⟨i, j⟩) = Bool.true then 1 else 0;
  ProbabilityTheory.iIndepFun Y (KqR75.ReserveRandom.law V p) ∧
    (∀ (i : I), Measurable (Y i)) ∧
      ∀ (i : I),
        ProbabilityTheory.HasLaw (Y i) (ProbabilityTheory.bernoulliMeasure 1 0 a)
          (KqR75.ReserveRandom.law V p)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ReserveScales] KqR75.ReserveRandom.eventually_reserve

  (u v : Fin n) : ¬2 ≤ q - 1 → False
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ReserveScales] KqR75.ReserveRandom.eventually_reserve_counts
KqR75.ReserveRandom.eventually_reserve_counts (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) {K c z : ℝ} (hK : 0 < K) (hc : 0 < c)
  (hz : 0 < z) (hgap : (2 * ↑q + 1) * z < 1) :
  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    ∃ p ω,
      ↑p = K * ↑n ^ (-z) ∧
        KqR75.ReserveRandom.RootGood p ω (q - 1) ∧
          (∀ (S : Finset (Fin n)) (l : ℕ),
              0 < l →
                l ≤ q - 1 →
                  c * ↑n ^ (1 - 2 * z) ≤ ↑S.card →
                    (↑S.card / (4 * ↑l)) ^ l * (↑p / 2) ^ l.choose 2 ≤
                      ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.cliquesIn (KqR75.ReserveRandom.adj ω) S l).card) ∧
            (∀ (u v : Fin n),
                u ≠ v →
                  have l := q - 2;
                  (↑n * ↑p ^ 2 / (8 * ↑l)) ^ l * (↑p / 2) ^ l.choose 2 ≤
                    ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.rootedExtensions (KqR75.ReserveRandom.adj ω) Finset.univ u v
                          l).card) ∧
              ∀ (H : Finset (Fin n)) (k : ℕ),
                H.card + k ≤ q →
                  ↑(KqR75.ReserveCuts.extensions (KqR75.ReserveRandom.adj ω) Finset.univ H k).card ≤
                    (2 * ↑n) ^ k * ↑p ^ (H.card * k + k.choose 2)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ReserveScales] _private.ReserveScales.0.KqR75.ReserveRandom.eventually_clique_budget
KqR75.ReserveRandom.eventually_clique_budget✝ (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) {K D z : ℝ} (hK : 0 < K) (hD : 0 < D)
  (hz : 0 < z) (hgap : (2 * ↑q + 1) * z < 1) :
  have c := D * (K / 2) ^ (q - 2) / (8 * ↑q ^ 2);
  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    have p := K * ↑n ^ (-z);
    have t := ⌈c * ↑n ^ (1 - ↑q * z)⌉₊;
    0 < t ∧
      ∀ (l : ℕ),
        0 < l →
          l ≤ q - 1 →
            ∀ (m : ℝ), D * ↑n ^ (1 - 2 * z) ≤ m → 2 * ↑l ≤ m ∧ ↑l * ↑t ≤ m / (4 * ↑l) * (p / 2) ^ (l - 1)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[ReserveTails] KqR75.ReserveTails.independent_bernoulli_sum
KqR75.ReserveTails.independent_bernoulli_sum.{u_1, u_2} {Ω : Type u_1} [MeasurableSpace Ω]
  {P : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure P] {ι : Type u_2} {Y : ι → Ω → ℝ}
  (p : ↑unitInterval) (s : Finset ι) (hI : ProbabilityTheory.iIndepFun Y P)
  (hM : ∀ (i : ι), Measurable (Y i))
  (hY : ∀ (i : ι), ProbabilityTheory.HasLaw (Y i) (ProbabilityTheory.bernoulliMeasure 1 0 p) P) (t : ℝ) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun ω => Real.exp (t * (∑ i ∈ s, Y i) ω)) P ∧
    ProbabilityTheory.mgf (∑ i ∈ s, Y i) P t ≤ Real.exp (↑s.card * ↑p * (Real.exp t - 1))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
