[ResidualSeed] KqR75.ResidualSeed.repair_after_deletion
KqR75.ResidualSeed.repair_after_deletion.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hn : 2 * q ^ 2 ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C) (ref : C) (d : ℕ) (s G : ℝ) (hs : 0 < s)
  (hG : 0 ≤ G) (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), d + q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i)
  (hdn : s * ↑n ≤ ↑d) (g : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ)
  (hg : ∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (v : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q), |g j v| ≤ G)
  (hcoord : ∀ (i j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), g j ↑i = if i = j then 1 else 0)
  (hmargin :
    ∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (i₁ i₂ : C),
      ∑ v, g j v * ↑(KqR75.SlotPairMargins.orderedCount v i₁ i₂) = 0)
  (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t M D δ : ℝ) (ht : 0 ≤ t) (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hD : 0 ≤ D)
  (hδ : 0 < δ) (hdeg : ∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ f, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑f) v ≤ t)
  (hsmall : 4 * (1 + ↑(q.choose 2)) * KqR75.SparseGram.outsideBound n q t ≤ 1 / 2)
  (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ)
  (hw : ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q), |w b| ≤ M * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q)
  (hwpos : ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q), δ ≤ w b)
  (hseed :
    ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) w e = 1)
  (hcompat : KqR75.ProfileBalance.compatible colour J m fun x => 1)
  (htarget :
    ∀ (i : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), |m ↑i - KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour w ↑i| ≤ D) :
  have α := KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q;
  have Q := G * ↑q.factorial ^ Fintype.card C / s ^ q / ↑n ^ 2;
  have u := M * (4 * ↑q ^ 2 * t / ↑n);
  have v := Q * (4 * ↑q ^ 2 * t / ↑n);
  have r := ↑(q.choose 2);
  have k := ↑(Fintype.card (KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref));
  have B := k * (Q * α + 8 * r * α * v);
  have T := D + M * ↑Jᶜ.card + 8 * ↑J.card * u;
  have E := 8 * r * α * u + B * (8 * T);
  k * (Q * ↑Jᶜ.card + 8 * ↑J.card * v) ≤ 1 / 2 →
    E ≤ δ / 2 →
      ∃ w',
        ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w' = fun x => 1) ∧
          KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w' = m ∧
            (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), 0 < w' b) ∧
              ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), |w' b - w ↑b| ≤ E
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ} (colour colour✝ : Fin n → C) (e_colour : colour = colour✝)

[ResidualSeed] KqR75.ResidualSeed.thresholds_of_degree
KqR75.ResidualSeed.thresholds_of_degree {n q : ℕ} (hn : 6 ≤ n) (hq : 3 ≤ q)
  (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t B₀ k M D : ℝ) (ht : 0 ≤ t) (hB₀ : 0 ≤ B₀) (hk : 0 ≤ k)
  (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hD : 0 ≤ D) (hdeg : ∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ f, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑f) v ≤ t)
  (hDcard : D ≤ ↑n * t / 2) :
  have ξ := t / ↑n;
  have α := KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q;
  have Q := B₀ / ↑n ^ 2;
  have u := M * (4 * ↑q ^ 2 * t / ↑n);
  have v := Q * (4 * ↑q ^ 2 * t / ↑n);
  have r := ↑(q.choose 2);
  have B := k * (Q * α + 8 * r * α * v);
  have T := D + M * ↑Jᶜ.card + 8 * ↑J.card * u;
  ξ ≤ 1 →
    4 * (1 + r) * KqR75.SparseGram.outsideBound n q t ≤ 16 * (1 + r) * ↑q ^ 2 * ξ ∧
      k * (Q * ↑Jᶜ.card + 8 * ↑J.card * v) ≤ k * B₀ * (1 / 2 + 16 * ↑q ^ 2) * ξ ∧
        8 * r * α * u + B * (8 * T) ≤
          α * ξ *
            (32 * r * M * ↑q ^ 2 + 8 * k * B₀ * (1 + 32 * r * ↑q ^ 2) * ((1 + M) / 2 + 16 * M * ↑q ^ 2))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  {s : Finset ι} [AddCommMonoid M] (p : ι → Prop) [DecidablePred p] (f : ι → M) :

[ResidualSeed] KqR75.ResidualSeed.repair_after_packing
KqR75.ResidualSeed.repair_after_packing.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hn : 2 * q ^ 2 ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C) (ref : C) (d : ℕ) (s G : ℝ) (hs : 0 < s)
  (hG : 0 ≤ G) (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), d + q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i)
  (hdn : s * ↑n ≤ ↑d) (g : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ)
  (hg : ∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (v : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q), |g j v| ≤ G)
  (hcoord : ∀ (i j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), g j ↑i = if i = j then 1 else 0)
  (hmargin :
    ∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (i₁ i₂ : C),
      ∑ v, g j v * ↑(KqR75.SlotPairMargins.orderedCount v i₁ i₂) = 0)
  (P : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t M δ : ℝ)
  (ht : 0 ≤ t) (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hδ : 0 < δ)
  (hpacking :
    ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      ↑{b ∈ P | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card = if e ∈ J then 0 else 1)
  (hdeg : ∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ f, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑f) v ≤ t)
  (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ)
  (hw : ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q), |w b| ≤ M * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q)
  (hwpos : ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q), δ ≤ w b)
  (hseed :
    ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) w e = 1) :
  have ξ := t / ↑n;
  have α := KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q;
  have B₀ := G * ↑q.factorial ^ Fintype.card C / s ^ q;
  have k := ↑(Fintype.card (KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref));
  have r := ↑(q.choose 2);
  have E :=
    α * ξ * (32 * r * M * ↑q ^ 2 + 8 * k * B₀ * (1 + 32 * r * ↑q ^ 2) * ((1 + M) / 2 + 16 * M * ↑q ^ 2));
  ξ ≤ 1 →
    16 * (1 + r) * ↑q ^ 2 * ξ ≤ 1 / 2 →
      k * B₀ * (1 / 2 + 16 * ↑q ^ 2) * ξ ≤ 1 / 2 →
        E ≤ δ / 2 →
          ∃ w',
            ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w' = fun x => 1) ∧
              (KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w' = fun i =>
                  KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour w i -
                    KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour (fun b => if b ∈ P then 1 else 0) i) ∧
                (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), 0 < w' b) ∧
                  ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)), |w' b - w ↑b| ≤ E
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

KqR75.ScalarDrift.close_budget (r m k Z γ X L s C δ η W D : ℝ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hm : 1 ≤ m) (hk : 0 < k)

[ScaleLP] KqR75.scale_optimal
KqR75.scale_optimal (c q : ℝ) (hc : 0 ≤ c) (hq : 3 ≤ q) :
  have D := c * (2 * q - 1) + 2;
  KqR75.ScaleFeasible c q ((2 * q - 1) / D) (2 / D) (2 / D) (1 / D) (1 / D) ∧
    ∀ (a u v δ z : ℝ), KqR75.ScaleFeasible c q a u v δ z → z ≤ 1 / D

[SeedScales] _private.SeedScales.0.KqR75.SeedScales.eventually_repair_after_packing._proof_1_1
KqR75.SeedScales.eventually_repair_after_packing._proof_1_1✝ (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (n : ℕ) (hnq : q ≤ n) :
  ¬0 < n → False
Axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

[SeedScales] KqR75.SeedScales.eventually_repair_after_packing
KqR75.SeedScales.eventually_repair_after_packing.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] (q : ℕ)
  (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ref : C) (s G M a D z : ℝ) (hs : 0 < s) (hG : 0 ≤ G) (hM : 0 ≤ M) (ha : 0 < a)
  (hz : 0 < z) :
  ∃ L,
    0 ≤ L ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (d : ℕ),
          (∀ (i : C), d + q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i) →
            s * ↑n ≤ ↑d →
              ∀ (g : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref → KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℝ),
                (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (v : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
                    |g j v| ≤ G) →
                  (∀ (i j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), g j ↑i = if i = j then 1 else 0) →
                    (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) (i₁ i₂ : C),
                        ∑ v, g j v * ↑(KqR75.SlotPairMargins.orderedCount v i₁ i₂) = 0) →
                      ∀ (P : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q))
                        (J : Finset ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (t : ℝ),
                        0 ≤ t →
                          t ≤ D * ↑n ^ (1 - z) →
                            (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                                ↑{b ∈ P | e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q b}.card =
                                  if e ∈ J then 0 else 1) →
                              (∀ (v : Fin n), ∑ f, KqR75.CliqueGram.incidence 2 (↑f) v ≤ t) →
                                ∀ (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ),
                                  (∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
                                      |w b| ≤ M * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q) →
                                    (∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
                                        a * KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q ≤ w b) →
                                      (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                                          KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) w e =
                                            1) →
                                        ∃ w',
                                          ((KqR75.HostCorrection.hostIncidence n q J).mulVec w' = fun x =>
                                              1) ∧
                                            (KqR75.ProfileBalance.hostHistogram colour J w' = fun i =>
                                                KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour w i -
                                                  KqR75.ProfileBalance.histogram colour
                                                    (fun b => if b ∈ P then 1 else 0) i) ∧
                                              (∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
                                                  0 < w' b) ∧
                                                ∀ (b : ↥(KqR75.HostCorrection.availableBlocks n q J)),
                                                  |w' b - w ↑b| ≤
                                                    KqR75.HostCorrection.scale n q * (L * ↑n ^ (-z))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[SignedBlock] _private.SignedBlock.0.KqR75.SignedBlock.moment._simp_1_1
KqR75.SignedBlock.moment._simp_1_1✝.{u_1, u_4} {ι : Type u_1} {M : Type u_4} {s : Finset ι}
  [AddCommMonoid M] {f g : ι → M} : ∑ x ∈ s, f x + ∑ x ∈ s, g x = ∑ x ∈ s, (f x + g x)

[SignedBlockScalar] KqR75.SignedBlock.polynomial_nonnegative

[SignedBlockScalar] KqR75.SignedBlock.coefficient_expansion
KqR75.SignedBlock.coefficient_expansion (r S : ℝ) :
  KqR75.SignedBlock.coefficient r S =
    (S - (2 * r - 1)) ^ 3 + (4 * r - 2) * (S - (2 * r - 1)) ^ 2 +
        (2 * r - 1) * (r - 1) * (S - (2 * r - 1)) +
      r * (2 * r - 1)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[SignedBlockScalar] KqR75.SignedBlock.certificate_identity
KqR75.SignedBlock.certificate_identity (r S T : ℝ) :
  KqR75.SignedBlock.polynomial r S T * S ^ 2 =
    (2 * r - 1) * (S * T - r ^ 2) ^ 2 + KqR75.SignedBlock.coefficient r S * (S * T - r ^ 2) +
      r * (S - r) ^ 2 * ((2 * r - 1) * S + (2 * r - 1) * r)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (colour : Fin n → C) (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q) :

      ⟨KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour (↑b) i, KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType._proof_2 colour b i⟩,

[SlotProfiles] KqR75.SlotProfiles.cell_column
KqR75.SlotProfiles.cell_column.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {K : Type u_2}
  [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {q : ℕ} (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s) (ref : C)
  (u : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref) :
  ∃ tag r,
    KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType tag = ↑u ∧
      have z := fun v =>
        KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType s (fun x => Equiv.refl K) tag ref v -
          KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType s
            (fun i => if i = r then Equiv.swap 0 1 else Equiv.refl K) tag ref v;
      (∀ (v : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q),
          KqR75.SlotProfiles.degree ref ↑u ≤ KqR75.SlotProfiles.degree ref v →
            z v = if ↑u = v then 1 else 0) ∧
        ∀ (a : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ) (P : Finset (Fin q × Fin q)),
          (∀ ij ∈ P, ij.1 ≠ ij.2) →
            ∀ (f : C → C → ℤ),
              (∀ (labels : Fin q → C),
                  a (KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType labels) = ∑ ij ∈ P, f (labels ij.1) (labels ij.2)) →
                ∑ v, z v * a v = 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[SlotProfiles] KqR75.SlotProfiles.cell_high_coordinates
KqR75.SlotProfiles.cell_high_coordinates.{u_1, u_2} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  {K : Type u_2} [Field K] [Fintype K] [DecidableEq K] {q : ℕ} (s : Fin q → K) (hs : Function.Injective s)
  (ref : C) :
  ∃ tag r L,
    (∀ (j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref), KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType (tag j) = ↑j) ∧
      ∀ (i j : KqR75.SlotProfiles.High C q ref),
        ∑ k,
            L j k *
              (KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType s (fun x => Equiv.refl K)
                  (tag k) ref ↑i -
                KqR75.AffineCellCounts.histogram KqR75.SlotProfiles.slotType s
                  (fun a => if a = r k then Equiv.swap 0 1 else Equiv.refl K) (tag k) ref ↑i) =
          if i = j then 1 else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TimeEnergy] KqR75.TimeEnergy.mean_error_after
KqR75.TimeEnergy.mean_error_after.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)) (k : ℝ) :
  ∑ c,
      KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h c *
        ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J (Option.some c :: h) k e =
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h * ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e -
        ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e -
      ∑ c,
        KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h c *
          KqR75.TimeEnergy.remainingLoss I (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h) c
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TimeEnergy] KqR75.TimeEnergy.physical_mean_drift
KqR75.TimeEnergy.physical_mean_drift.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)) (r : ℕ)
  (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (k η : ℝ) :
  have w := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h;
  have x := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k;
  have Z := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h;
  have M := ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e * x e;
  ∑ c,
      w c *
        (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J (Option.some c :: h) (k - η) -
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J h k) =
    ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e * x e ^ 2 - 2 * ∑ b, w b * (∑ e ∈ I b, x e) ^ 2 -
              2 * (↑r - 1) * k * M +
            2 * ∑ e, KqR75.OverlapDrift.correction I w e * x e +
          ∑ c, w c * KqR75.MeanEnergy.squareLoss I w c +
        2 * η * (Z * ∑ e, x e - M - ∑ c, w c * KqR75.TimeEnergy.remainingLoss I w c) +
      η ^ 2 * (Z * (↑(KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining I J h).card - ↑r))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeCountHorizon] KqR75.TypeCountHorizon.compensator_error
KqR75.TypeCountHorizon.compensator_error.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (w : B), 0 ≤ μ w)
  (J : Finset E) (S : Finset B) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (η : ℝ) (h : List (Option B))
  (hlive : ∀ j < h.length, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard (List.drop (h.length - j) h))
  (hprop :
    ∀ j < h.length,
      |KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S (List.drop (h.length - j) h) -
            KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S []| ≤
        η) :
  |LatinonEntropy.pathSum
          (fun g x =>
            ∑ w,
              (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard).transition g w * KqR75.TypeCounts.indicator S w)
          h -
        ↑h.length * KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S []| ≤
    ↑h.length * η
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeCountHorizon] KqR75.TypeCountHorizon.simultaneous
KqR75.TypeCountHorizon.simultaneous.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] {D : Type u_3} (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ)
  (hμ : ∀ (w : B), 0 ≤ μ w) (J : Finset E) (S : Finset D) (F : D → Finset B) (r T : ℕ) (M n β u η v δ ε : ℝ)
  (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hM : 0 < M) (hT : ↑T < M) (hsize : ∀ (w : B), (I w).card = r)
  (hJ : ↑J.card = ↑r * M)
  (hguards :
    ∀ (h : List (Option B)),
      h.length < T →
        KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h →
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
                (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M) h <
              u →
            KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M n β h) :
  have Y :=
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
      (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M);
  have guard := fun h => KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M n β h ∧ h.length < T ∧ Y h < u;
  have K := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard;
  KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T (KqR75.EnergyExit.crossed Y u) ≤ 1 / 4 →
    (∀ d ∈ S,
        ∀ t ≤ T,
          (KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K t fun h =>
              η <
                |KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J (F d) h -
                    KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J (F d) []|) ≤
            δ) →
      (∀ d ∈ S,
          (KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T fun h =>
              v <
                |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count (F d) h) -
                    LatinonEntropy.pathSum
                      (fun g x => ∑ w, K.transition g w * KqR75.TypeCounts.indicator (F d) w) h|) ≤
            ε) →
        ↑S.card * (↑T + 1) * δ + ↑S.card * ε ≤ 1 / 4 →
          1 / 2 ≤
            KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T fun h =>
              Option.none ∉ h ∧
                KqR75.WeightedGreedy.valid I J h ∧
                  ¬KqR75.EnergyExit.crossed Y u h ∧
                    ∀ d ∈ S,
                      |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count (F d) h) -
                            ↑T * KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J (F d) []| ≤
                        v + ↑T * η
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeCounts] KqR75.TypeCounts.count.eq_3
KqR75.TypeCounts.count.eq_3.{u_2} {B : Type u_2} [DecidableEq B] (S : Finset B) (b : B)
  (h : List (Option B)) :
  KqR75.TypeCounts.count S (Option.some b :: h) = KqR75.TypeCounts.count S h + if b ∈ S then 1 else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeCounts] KqR75.TypeCounts.tail
KqR75.TypeCounts.tail.{u_2} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B]
  (K : LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel (Option B)) (S : Finset B) (T : ℕ) (hT : 0 < T) (u : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) :
  (KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T fun h =>
      u ≤
        |↑(KqR75.TypeCounts.count S h) -
            LatinonEntropy.pathSum (fun h x => ∑ a, K.transition h a * KqR75.TypeCounts.indicator S a) h|) ≤
    2 * Real.exp (-(u ^ 2 / (2 * ↑T)))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeCounts] KqR75.TypeCounts.count.eq_def
KqR75.TypeCounts.count.eq_def.{u_2} {B : Type u_2} [DecidableEq B] (S : Finset B) (x✝ : List (Option B)) :
  KqR75.TypeCounts.count S x✝ =
    match x✝ with
    | [] => 0
    | Option.none :: h => KqR75.TypeCounts.count S h
    | Option.some b :: h => KqR75.TypeCounts.count S h + if b ∈ S then 1 else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  [NonUnitalNonAssocSemiring R] (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → R) (a : R) : ∑ i ∈ s, f i * a = (∑ i ∈ s, f i) * a

  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (w : B → ℝ) (r : ℕ) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) :

[TypeCovariance] KqR75.TypeCovariance.mean_bound
KqR75.TypeCovariance.mean_bound.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (w : B → ℝ) (hw : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ w b) (S : Finset B)
  (r : ℕ) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (p k γ : ℝ) (hp0 : 0 ≤ p) (hp1 : p ≤ 1) (hγ : 0 ≤ γ)
  (hmass : ∑ b, KqR75.TypeWeights.marked S w b = p * ∑ b, w b)
  (hover : ∀ (b : B), KqR75.OverlapDrift.overcount I w b ≤ γ) :
  |∑ c,
        w c *
          (p * KqR75.TypeWeights.removed I w c -
            KqR75.TypeWeights.removed I (KqR75.TypeWeights.marked S w) c)| ≤
    ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e * |KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e - k| + γ * ∑ b, w b
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeCovariance] KqR75.TypeCovariance.mean_identity
KqR75.TypeCovariance.mean_identity.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (w v : B → ℝ) (r : ℕ) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r)
  (p k : ℝ) (hmass : ∑ b, v b = p * ∑ b, w b) :
  ∑ c, w c * (p * KqR75.TypeWeights.removed I w c - KqR75.TypeWeights.removed I v c) =
    -∑ e,
          (KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I v e - p * KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e) *
            (KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e - k) +
      ∑ b, (v b - p * w b) * KqR75.OverlapDrift.overcount I w b
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeCovariance] KqR75.TypeWeights.removed.congr_simp

  (e_c : c = c✝) : KqR75.TypeWeights.removed I w c = KqR75.TypeWeights.removed I✝ w✝ c✝
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeDrift] KqR75.TypeDrift.mean_ratio
KqR75.TypeDrift.mean_ratio.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B]
  [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (w : B → ℝ) (hw : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ w b) (S : Finset B) (r : ℕ)
  (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e ≤ 1)
  (k γ : ℝ) (hγ : 0 ≤ γ) (hover : ∀ (b : B), KqR75.OverlapDrift.overcount I w b ≤ γ)
  (hZ : 2 * ↑r ≤ ∑ b, w b) :
  have Z := ∑ b, w b;
  have A := ∑ b, KqR75.TypeWeights.marked S w b;
  |∑ c,
        w c / Z *
          ((A - KqR75.TypeWeights.removed I (KqR75.TypeWeights.marked S w) c) /
              (Z - KqR75.TypeWeights.removed I w c) -
            A / Z)| ≤
    (∑ e, KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e * |KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I w e - k|) / Z ^ 2 + γ / Z +
      2 * ↑r ^ 2 / Z ^ 2
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeDrift] KqR75.TypeDrift.physical_mean
KqR75.TypeDrift.physical_mean.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (S : Finset B) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (h : List (Option B))
  (hl : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard h) (r : ℕ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1) (k γ : ℝ) (hk : 0 ≤ k) (hγ : 0 ≤ γ)
  (hover : ∀ (b : B), KqR75.OverlapDrift.overcount I (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h) b ≤ γ)
  (hZ : 2 * ↑r ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h) :
  |∑ a,
        (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard).transition h a *
          (KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S (a :: h) - KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S h)| ≤
    (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J h k + k * ∑ e, |KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e|) /
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h ^ 2 +
        γ / KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h +
      2 * ↑r ^ 2 / KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h ^ 2
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeDriftEnvelope] KqR75.TypeDriftEnvelope.absolute_error
KqR75.TypeDriftEnvelope.absolute_error {n p r C A m X L : ℝ} (hn : 0 < n) (hp : 0 < p) (hr : 0 ≤ r)
  (hC : 0 ≤ C) (hA : 0 ≤ A) (_hm : 0 ≤ m) (hX : 0 ≤ X) (hmul : m ≤ C * n ^ 2 * p)
  (henergy : X ≤ A * n * p ^ (-2 * r)) (hL : L ^ 2 ≤ r * m * X) :

[TypeDriftEnvelope] KqR75.TypeDriftEnvelope.active_bound
KqR75.TypeDriftEnvelope.active_bound.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (S : Finset B) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (r T : ℕ) (M n β b c C A : ℝ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hn : 1 ≤ n)
  (hc : 0 < c) (hC : 0 ≤ C) (hA : 0 ≤ A) (hMₗ : c * n ^ 2 ≤ M) (hMᵤ : M ≤ C * n ^ 2) (hT : ↑T < M)
  (hsize : ∀ (w : B), (I w).card = r) (hJ : ↑J.card = ↑r * M)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) (hb : 0 ≤ b) (hβrate : β ≤ b / n)
  (hcodeg : ∀ (e f : E), e ≠ f → KqR75.CodegreeLoss.codegree I μ e f ≤ β) (h : List (Option B))
  (hl : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard h) (hlen : h.length ≤ T)
  (hreg : KqR75.StoppedPhysical.regular I μ J r M n β h)
  (hY :
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (r - 1) M)
        (KqR75.StoppedPhysical.profile (2 * r) M) h ≤
      A * n)
  (hZ : 2 * ↑r ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h) :
  have p := (M - ↑(KqR75.WeightedGreedy.selectedCount h)) / M;
  |∑ x,
        (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard).transition h x *
          KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment I μ J S guard h x| ≤
    (2 / c) ^ 2 * A * (n ^ (-3) * p ^ (-4 * ↑r)) +
          (2 / c) ^ 2 * √(↑r * C * A) * (n ^ (-5 / 2) * p ^ (-2 * ↑r - 1 / 2)) +
        2 / c * ↑r ^ 2 * b * (n ^ (-3) * p ^ (-↑r)) +
      (2 / c) ^ 2 * (2 * ↑r ^ 2) * (n ^ (-4) * p ^ (-2 * ↑r))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeDriftEnvelope] _private.TypeDriftEnvelope.0.KqR75.TypeDriftEnvelope.rpow_sq
KqR75.TypeDriftEnvelope.rpow_sq✝ {x : ℝ} (hx : 0 ≤ x) (s : ℝ) : (x ^ s) ^ 2 = x ^ (2 * s)

[TypeDriftEnvelope] KqR75.TypeDriftEnvelope.drift
KqR75.TypeDriftEnvelope.drift {n p r c C A X L Z γ b : ℝ} (hn : 0 < n) (hp : 0 < p) (_hr : 0 ≤ r)
  (hc : 0 < c) (hA : 0 ≤ A) (hb : 0 ≤ b) (_hX : 0 ≤ X) (_hL : 0 ≤ L) (_hγ : 0 ≤ γ)
  (henergy : X ≤ A * n * p ^ (-2 * r)) (herror : L ≤ √(r * C * A) * n ^ (3 / 2) * p ^ (1 / 2 - r))
  (hmass : c / 2 * n ^ 2 * p ^ r ≤ Z) (hcodeg : γ ≤ r ^ 2 * b / n) :
  (X + p ^ (r - 1) * L) / Z ^ 2 + γ / Z + 2 * r ^ 2 / Z ^ 2 ≤
    (2 / c) ^ 2 * A * (n ^ (-3) * p ^ (-4 * r)) +
          (2 / c) ^ 2 * √(r * C * A) * (n ^ (-5 / 2) * p ^ (-2 * r - 1 / 2)) +
        2 / c * r ^ 2 * b * (n ^ (-3) * p ^ (-r)) +
      (2 / c) ^ 2 * (2 * r ^ 2) * (n ^ (-4) * p ^ (-2 * r))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeMartingale] KqR75.TypeMartingale.tail
KqR75.TypeMartingale.tail.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B]
  [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E) (S : Finset B)
  (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (r : ℕ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1) (g : ℕ → ℝ) (hg : ∀ (i : ℕ), 0 < g i)
  (hregular : ∀ (h : List (Option B)), guard h → 2 * ↑r ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h)
  (hdenom : ∀ (h : List (Option B)), guard h → g h.length ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h)
  (T : ℕ) (u : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (hV : 0 < ∑ i ∈ Finset.range T, (4 * ↑r / g i) ^ 2) :
  have K := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard;
  have Z := KqR75.FiniteAzuma.centered K (KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment I μ J S guard);
  (KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T fun h => u ≤ |LatinonEntropy.pathSum Z h|) ≤
    2 * Real.exp (-(u ^ 2 / (2 * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range T, (4 * ↑r / g i) ^ 2)))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeMartingale] KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment.eq_2

  [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (S : Finset B)
  (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (h : List (Option B)) (c : B) :
  KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment I μ J S guard h (Option.some c) =
    if KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard h then

    else 0

[TypeTails] KqR75.TypeTails.eventually_prefix_budget
KqR75.TypeTails.eventually_prefix_budget (k : ℕ) {C κ η ε : ℝ} (hκ : 0 < κ) (hη : 0 < η) (hε : 0 < ε) :

[TypeTracking] KqR75.TypeTracking.decomposition
KqR75.TypeTracking.decomposition.{u_3} {Ω : Type u_3} [Fintype Ω] (K : LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel Ω)
  (F : List Ω → Ω → ℝ) (h : List Ω) :
  LatinonEntropy.pathSum F h =
    LatinonEntropy.pathSum (KqR75.FiniteAzuma.centered K F) h +
      LatinonEntropy.pathSum (fun h x => ∑ a, K.transition h a * F h a) h
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeTracking] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel.congr_simp
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel.congr_simp.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  {inst✝ : DecidableEq E} [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I I✝ : B → Finset E) (e_I : I = I✝)
  (μ μ✝ : B → ℝ) (e_μ : μ = μ✝) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J J✝ : Finset E) (e_J : J = J✝)
  (guard guard✝ : List (Option B) → Prop) (e_guard : guard = guard✝) :
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard =
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I✝ μ✝ (Eq.ndrec (motive := fun μ => ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) hμ e_μ) J✝ guard✝
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeTracking] KqR75.TypeTracking.observed_tail
KqR75.TypeTracking.observed_tail.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (S : Finset B) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (r : ℕ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1) (g : ℕ → ℝ) (hg : ∀ (i : ℕ), 0 < g i)
  (hregular : ∀ (h : List (Option B)), guard h → 2 * ↑r ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h)
  (hdenom : ∀ (h : List (Option B)), guard h → g h.length ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h)
  (T : ℕ) (u : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (hV : 0 < ∑ i ∈ Finset.range T, (4 * ↑r / g i) ^ 2) :
  have K := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard;
  have F := KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment I μ J S guard;
  have drift := LatinonEntropy.pathSum fun h x => ∑ a, K.transition h a * F h a;
  (KqR75.FiniteProcess.eventMass K T fun h =>
      u ≤ |KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S h - KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S [] - drift h|) ≤
    2 * Real.exp (-(u ^ 2 / (2 * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range T, (4 * ↑r / g i) ^ 2)))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeTracking] KqR75.TypeTracking.physical_decomposition
KqR75.TypeTracking.physical_decomposition.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b)
  (J : Finset E) (S : Finset B) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (h : List (Option B))
  (hp : 0 < LatinonEntropy.historyWeight (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard) h) :
  have K := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard;
  have F := KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment I μ J S guard;
  KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S h - KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S [] =
    LatinonEntropy.pathSum (KqR75.FiniteAzuma.centered K F) h +
      LatinonEntropy.pathSum (fun h x => ∑ a, K.transition h a * F h a) h
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[TypeTracking] KqR75.TypeTracking.positive_increment
KqR75.TypeTracking.positive_increment.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (S : Finset B) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (h : List (Option B)) (a : Option B)
  (hp : 0 < (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard).transition h a) :
  KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment I μ J S guard h a =
    KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S (a :: h) - KqR75.TypeWeights.proportion I μ J S h

[TypeTracking] KqR75.TypeTracking.telescope
KqR75.TypeTracking.telescope.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]

[TypeTracking] KqR75.TypeTracking.active_mean_bound
KqR75.TypeTracking.active_mean_bound.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (S : Finset B) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (h : List (Option B))
  (hl : KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard h) (r : ℕ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r)
  (hload : ∀ (e : E), KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load I μ e ≤ 1) (k γ : ℝ) (hk : 0 ≤ k) (hγ : 0 ≤ γ)
  (hover : ∀ (b : B), KqR75.OverlapDrift.overcount I (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h) b ≤ γ)
  (hZ : 2 * ↑r ≤ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h) :
  |∑ a,
        (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard).transition h a *
          KqR75.TypeMartingale.increment I μ J S guard h a| ≤
    (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J h k + k * ∑ e, |KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e|) /
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h ^ 2 +
        γ / KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h +
      2 * ↑r ^ 2 / KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h ^ 2
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

KqR75.UpperCounting.log_designFibre_upper_with_envelope._proof_1_4✝.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ} (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ)
  (hr : 3 ≤ q.choose 2) : ¬1 ≤ q.choose 2 → False
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperDesignFibre] KqR75.UpperCounting.log_designFibre_upper_with_envelope
KqR75.UpperCounting.log_designFibre_upper_with_envelope.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  {n q : ℕ} (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hnq : q ≤ n) (colour : Fin n → C)
  (hclasses : ∀ (i : C), q ≤ KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ i)
  (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ)
  (hmargin :
    ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
        ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliques colour Finset.univ (KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e)))
  (hfibre : (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).Nonempty) (W : ℝ) (hW : 0 ≤ W)
  (hsize : 4 * ↑(q.choose 3) * W + 1 ≤ ↑n)
  (hw :
    ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q),
      KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) (fun t => ↑(m t)) b ≤
        W / ↑n ^ (q - 2)) :
  have r := q.choose 2;
  have s := ↑(r - 1);
  have B := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r;
  have K := 4 * ↑(q.choose 3) * W + 1;
  Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card +
      ∑ t,
        ↑(m t) *
          Real.log
            (↑(m t) / ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) t).card) ≤
    -s * B + K ^ (1 / s) * (Real.log 2 + s + 1 / (s - 1)) * ↑n ^ (2 - 1 / s)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperDesignFibre] _private.UpperDesignFibre.0.KqR75.UpperCounting.reference_score_of_fibre
KqR75.UpperCounting.reference_score_of_fibre✝.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C] {n q : ℕ}
  (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ)
  (P : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) (hP : P ∈ KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m) :
  ∑ b ∈ P,
      Real.log (KqR75.ProfileWeights.weight (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) (fun t => ↑(m t)) b) =
    ∑ t,
      ↑(m t) *
        Real.log (↑(m t) / ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) t).card)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperEntropyIntegral] KqR75.UpperCounting.weighted_entropy_integral
KqR75.UpperCounting.weighted_entropy_integral (r : ℕ) (hr : 0 < r) (h s : ℝ) (hh : 0 < h) (hh₁ : h ≤ 1)

[UpperEntropyRate] KqR75.UpperCounting.eventually_card_designFibre_upper_entropy
KqR75.UpperCounting.eventually_card_designFibre_upper_entropy.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C]
  [DecidableEq C] (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ρ : ℝ) (hρ : 0 < ρ) :
  ∃ err,
    0 ≤ err ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ),
          (∀ (c : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c)) →
            (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
                  ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliques colour Finset.univ
                      (KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e))) →
              have r := q.choose 2;
              have s := ↑(r - 1);
              have B := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r;
              have N := fun t =>
                ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour) t).card;
              have K := ∑ t, ↑(m t) / B * Real.log (↑(m t) / B / (N t / ↑(n.choose q)));
              ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ≤
                Real.exp (B * (Real.log ↑((n - 2).choose (q - 2)) - s - K) + err * ↑n ^ (2 - 1 / s))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperEntropyRate] KqR75.UpperCounting.eventually_card_designFibre_upper
KqR75.UpperCounting.eventually_card_designFibre_upper.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ρ : ℝ) (hρ : 0 < ρ) :
  ∃ err,
    0 ≤ err ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ),
          (∀ (c : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c)) →
            (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
                  ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliques colour Finset.univ
                      (KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e))) →
              have r := q.choose 2;
              have s := ↑(r - 1);
              have B := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r;
              ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card ≤
                Real.exp
                  (-∑ t,
                          ↑(m t) *
                            Real.log
                              (↑(m t) /
                                ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour)
                                      t).card) -
                      s * B +
                    err * ↑n ^ (2 - 1 / s))
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperExceptional] _private.UpperExceptional.0.KqR75.UpperCounting.exceptional_card_le._proof_1_6

  (P : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)) (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))

    (((fun j => {b ∈ P | (↑e ∩ ↑b).card = j}) 2).biUnion (KqR75.UpperCounting.overlapCandidates e)).card ≤
      1 * (q.choose 3 * n ^ (q - 3)))

      (((fun j => {b ∈ P | (↑e ∩ ↑b).card = j}) 2).biUnion (KqR75.UpperCounting.overlapCandidates e) ∪
            ((fun j => {b ∈ P | (↑e ∩ ↑b).card = j}) 1).biUnion
              (KqR75.UpperCounting.overlapCandidates e)).card +
        (((fun j => {b ∈ P | (↑e ∩ ↑b).card = j}) 0).biUnion
            (KqR75.UpperCounting.overlapCandidates e)).card)
  (hcard' :
    (((fun j => {b ∈ P | (↑e ∩ ↑b).card = j}) 2).biUnion (KqR75.UpperCounting.overlapCandidates e) ∪
          ((fun j => {b ∈ P | (↑e ∩ ↑b).card = j}) 1).biUnion
            (KqR75.UpperCounting.overlapCandidates e)).card ≤
      (((fun j => {b ∈ P | (↑e ∩ ↑b).card = j}) 2).biUnion (KqR75.UpperCounting.overlapCandidates e)).card +
        (((fun j => {b ∈ P | (↑e ∩ ↑b).card = j}) 1).biUnion
            (KqR75.UpperCounting.overlapCandidates e)).card) :
  ¬(KqR75.UpperCounting.exceptional P e).card ≤ 4 * (q.choose 3 * n ^ (q - 3)) → False
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperFootprint] KqR75.UpperCounting.active_reference_score
KqR75.UpperCounting.active_reference_score {n q : ℕ} (hq : 2 ≤ q) (P : KqR75.UpperCounting.FullPacking n q)
  (key : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n) → ℕ) (hkey : Function.Injective key)
  (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ) :
  (∑ e,
      if KqR75.UpperCounting.active P.property key e then
        Real.log (w (KqR75.UpperCounting.owner P.property e))
      else 0) =
    ∑ b ∈ ↑P, Real.log (w b)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperFootprint] KqR75.UpperCounting.active.eq_1

  (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) :
  KqR75.UpperCounting.active hP key e =
    ∀ f ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q (KqR75.UpperCounting.owner hP e), key e ≤ key f

  (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (c : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)
  (he : e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q c) (hc : c ∉ KqR75.UpperCounting.exceptional (↑P) e) :
  (KqR75.UpperCounting.requiredEdges P e c).card = (q.choose 2 - 1) * q.choose 2 ∧
    Disjoint (KqR75.UpperCounting.requiredEdges P e c)

[UpperIntegralBound] KqR75.UpperCounting.weighted_upper_of_exceptional_mass

  (S : Finset (KqR75.UpperCounting.FullPacking n q)) (hS : S.Nonempty)
  (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ) (hw : ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q), 0 ≤ w b)

  (hload :
    ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
      KqR75.WeightedEnergy.load (KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q) w e = 1)
  (δ v : ℝ) (hδ : 0 < δ) (hδ₁ : δ ≤ 1)
  (hmass :
    ∀ P ∈ S, ∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)), ∑ b ∈ KqR75.UpperCounting.exceptional (↑P) e, w b ≤ δ)
  (hscore : ∀ P ∈ S, v ≤ ∑ b ∈ ↑P, Real.log (w b)) :
  have r := q.choose 2;
  have s := ↑(r - 1);

    ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r *
      (-s + δ ^ (1 / s) * (Real.log 2 + s + 1 / (s - 1)))

[UpperPriorityAverage] KqR75.UpperCounting.active.congr_simp
KqR75.UpperCounting.active.congr_simp {n q : ℕ} {P P✝ : Finset (KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q)}
  (e_P : P = P✝) (hP : KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.packs P Finset.univ)
  (key key✝ : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n) → ℕ) (e_key : key = key✝)
  (e e✝ : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) (e_e : e = e✝) :

[UpperPriorityAverage] KqR75.UpperCounting.activeLogReference.eq_1
KqR75.UpperCounting.activeLogReference.eq_1 {n q L : ℕ} (P : KqR75.UpperCounting.FullPacking n q)
  (lab : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n) → Fin L) (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n))
  (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ) :
  KqR75.UpperCounting.activeLogReference P lab e w =
    if
        KqR75.UpperCounting.active (KqR75.UpperCounting.activeLogReference._proof_1 P)
          (KqR75.UpperCounting.priorityKey lab) e then
      Real.log
        (∑ b ∈ KqR75.UpperCounting.availableCandidates P (KqR75.UpperCounting.priorityKey lab) e, w b)
    else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperUniform] KqR75.UpperCounting.eventually_log_designFibre_upper
KqR75.UpperCounting.eventually_log_designFibre_upper.{u_1} {C : Type u_1} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq C]
  (q : ℕ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (ρ : ℝ) (hρ : 0 < ρ) :
  ∃ err,
    0 ≤ err ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        ∀ (colour : Fin n → C) (m : KqR75.ColouredCliques.TypeProfile C q → ℤ),
          (∀ (c : C), ρ * ↑n ≤ ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour Finset.univ c)) →
            (∀ (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)),
                KqR75.ColouredCliques.pairMargin colour (fun t => ↑(m t)) e =
                  ↑(KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliques colour Finset.univ
                      (KqR75.ColouredCliques.profile colour ↑e))) →
              (KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).Nonempty →
                have r := q.choose 2;
                have s := ↑(r - 1);
                have B := ↑(Fintype.card ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) / ↑r;
                Real.log ↑(KqR75.PhysicalCompletion.designFibre colour m).card +
                    ∑ t,
                      ↑(m t) *
                        Real.log
                          (↑(m t) /
                            ↑(KqR75.ProfileWeights.fibre (KqR75.ColouredCliques.cliqueType colour)
                                  t).card) ≤
                  -s * B + err * ↑n ^ (2 - 1 / s)
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperWeightedReveal] KqR75.UpperCounting.availableCandidates.eq_1
KqR75.UpperCounting.availableCandidates.eq_1 {n q : ℕ} (P : KqR75.UpperCounting.FullPacking n q)
  (key : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n) → ℕ) (e : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n)) :
  KqR75.UpperCounting.availableCandidates P key e =
    {c |
      e ∈ KqR75.SparseGram.resources n q c ∧
        (c ∈ KqR75.UpperCounting.exceptional (↑P) e ∨
          ∀ g ∈ KqR75.UpperCounting.requiredEdges P e c, key e ≤ key g)}
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[UpperWeightedReveal] KqR75.UpperCounting.weighted_reveal_bound
KqR75.UpperCounting.weighted_reveal_bound {n q : ℕ} (hq : 3 ≤ q)
  (S : Finset (KqR75.UpperCounting.FullPacking n q)) (hS : S.Nonempty)
  (key : ↥(KqR75.CompleteJohnson.Edge n) → ℕ) (hkey : Function.Injective key)
  (w : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q → ℝ) (hw : ∀ (b : KqR75.ColouredCliques.Clique n q), 0 ≤ w b)
  (hwpos : ∀ P ∈ S, ∀ b ∈ ↑P, 0 < w b) :
  ↑S.card * Real.log ↑S.card ≤
    ∑ P ∈ S,
      ∑ e,
        if KqR75.UpperCounting.active P.property key e then
          Real.log (∑ b ∈ KqR75.UpperCounting.availableCandidates P key e, w b) -
            Real.log (w (KqR75.UpperCounting.owner P.property e))
        else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel.eq_1
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel.eq_1.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) :
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard =
    {
      transition := fun h a =>
        if KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard h then
          match a with
          | Option.none => 0
          | Option.some b =>
            KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h b / KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h
        else if a = Option.none then 1 else 0,
      nonneg := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel._proof_7 I μ hμ J guard,
      total := KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel._proof_8 I μ J guard }
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel._proof_8

  [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop)
  (h : List (Option B)) :
  (∑ a,
      if KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard h then
        match a with
        | Option.none => 0
        | Option.some b =>
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h b / KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h
      else if a = Option.none then 1 else 0) =
    1

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel._proof_7
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel._proof_7.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_2} {B : Type u_1} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (h : List (Option B)) (a : Option B) :
  0 ≤
    if KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard h then
      match a with
      | Option.none => 0
      | Option.some b =>
        KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h b / KqR75.WeightedGreedy.totalWeight I μ J h
    else if a = Option.none then 1 else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining I J h).card + r * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.selectedCount h = J.card

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining.eq_def
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining.eq_def.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [DecidableEq E]

  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining I J x✝ =

    | [] => J
    | Option.none :: h => KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining I J h
    | Option.some b :: h => KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining I J h \ I b

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error_after
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error_after.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)) (C : B)
  (k η : ℝ) (e : E) :
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J (Option.some C :: h) (k - η) e =
    if e ∈ I C then 0
    else
      KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e - KqR75.WeightedGreedy.loss I μ J h C e +
        if e ∈ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining I J h then η else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (h : List (Option B)) (h_1 : Unit → motive []) (h_2 : (h : List (Option B)) → motive (Option.none :: h))
  (h_3 : (b : B) → (h : List (Option B)) → motive (Option.some b :: h)) :
  (match Option.none :: h with
    | [] => h_1 ()
    | Option.none :: h => h_2 h
    | Option.some b :: h => h_3 b h) =
    h_2 h
Axioms: []

[WeightedGreedy] LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel.mk.congr_simp
LatinonEntropy.FiniteKernel.mk.congr_simp.{u_1} {Ω : Type u_1} [Fintype Ω]
  (transition transition✝ : List Ω → Ω → ℝ) (e_transition : transition = transition✝)
  (nonneg : ∀ (h : List Ω) (a : Ω), 0 ≤ transition h a) (total : ∀ (h : List Ω), ∑ a, transition h a = 1) :
  { transition := transition, nonneg := nonneg, total := total } =
    { transition := transition✝,
      nonneg :=
        Eq.ndrec (motive := fun transition => ∀ (h : List Ω) (a : Ω), 0 ≤ transition h a) nonneg
          e_transition,
      total :=
        Eq.ndrec (motive := fun transition => ∀ (h : List Ω), ∑ a, transition h a = 1) total e_transition }
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.loss_on_selected

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error.eq_1
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error.eq_1.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B]
  (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)) (k : ℝ) (e : E) :
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e =
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e - if e ∈ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining I J h then k else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy_after
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy_after.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (J : Finset E) (h : List (Option B)) (C : B)
  (k η : ℝ) :
  KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J (Option.some C :: h) (k - η) - KqR75.WeightedGreedy.energy I μ J h k =
    ∑ e,
      if e ∈ I C then -KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e ^ 2
      else
        -2 * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.loss I μ J h C e +
            KqR75.WeightedGreedy.loss I μ J h C e ^ 2 +
          if e ∈ KqR75.WeightedGreedy.remaining I J h then
            2 * η * (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e - KqR75.WeightedGreedy.loss I μ J h C e) + η ^ 2
          else 0
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.signed_energy
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.signed_energy.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E] [DecidableEq E]
  [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b) (J : Finset E)
  (r : ℕ) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hsize : ∀ (b : B), (I b).card = r) (h : List (Option B)) (k : ℝ) (hk : 0 < k) :
  ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.load I μ J h e * KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e ^ 2 -
      2 *
        ∑ b,
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.availableWeight I μ J h b *
            (∑ e ∈ I b, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e) ^ 2 ≤
    (2 * ↑r - 1) * k * ∑ e, KqR75.WeightedGreedy.error I μ J h k e ^ 2
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[WeightedGreedy] KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy_drift_bound
KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy_drift_bound.{u_1, u_2} {E : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [Fintype E]
  [DecidableEq E] [Fintype B] [DecidableEq B] (I : B → Finset E) (μ : B → ℝ) (hμ : ∀ (b : B), 0 ≤ μ b)
  (J : Finset E) (guard : List (Option B) → Prop) (k factor b : ℕ → ℝ) (hb : ∀ (i : ℕ), 0 ≤ b i)
  (hactive :
    ∀ (h : List (Option B)),
      KqR75.WeightedGreedy.live I μ J guard h →
        ∑ a,
            (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard).transition h a *
              KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J k factor (a :: h) ≤
          KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J k factor h + b h.length)
  (T : ℕ) :
  LatinonEntropy.historyExpectation (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.kernel I μ hμ J guard) T
      (KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J k factor) ≤
    KqR75.WeightedGreedy.stoppedEnergy I μ J k factor [] + ∑ s ∈ Finset.range T, b s
Axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

[WeightedGreedy] _private.WeightedGreedy.0.KqR75.WeightedGreedy.sum_load._simp_1_2

  [NonUnitalNonAssocSemiring R] (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → R) (a : R) : ∑ i ∈ s, f i * a = (∑ i ∈ s, f i) * a
